function v53EvidenceLedger(history=[]){
  const out=[],seen=new Set();
  for(const turn of arr(history).slice(-40)){
    const frame=turn?.resultContext?.frame||turn?.resultContext||{};
    const contexts=arr(frame?.contexts).length?arr(frame.contexts):[frame];
    for(const c of contexts){for(const snap of arr(c?.snapshots))for(const op of arr(snap?.operations)){
      const oid=trim(op?.operation_id);if(!oid)continue;const id=`EV_${oid}`;if(seen.has(id))continue;seen.add(id);
      const ev=op?.evidence||{},tables=arr(ev?.tables);
      out.push({id,operation:trim(op?.operation||op?.args?.operation),subject:trim(op?.args?.event||op?.args?.person||op?.args?.responsible),subjects:arr(op?.args?.events).map(trim).filter(Boolean).slice(0,8),available:true,table_keys:tables.map(t=>trim(t?.key)).filter(Boolean).slice(0,6),created_seq:num(snap?.created_seq)});
    }}
  }
  return out.slice(-12);
}
function v53MaterializeEvidence(history=[],ids=[]){
  const wanted=new Set(arr(ids).map(trim).filter(Boolean)),out={};if(!wanted.size)return out;
  for(const turn of arr(history).slice(-50)){
    const frame=turn?.resultContext?.frame||turn?.resultContext||{},contexts=arr(frame?.contexts).length?arr(frame.contexts):[frame];
    for(const c of contexts)for(const snap of arr(c?.snapshots))for(const op of arr(snap?.operations)){
      const id=`EV_${trim(op?.operation_id)}`;if(!wanted.has(id)||out[id]||!op?.evidence)continue;out[id]=op.evidence;
    }
  }
  return out;
}
function v53ThreadGraph(history=[],state={},selectedEventId=''){
  const latest=arr(history).slice(-1)[0]||{},persisted=latest?.resultContext?.thread_graph;if(persisted&&typeof persisted==='object'&&arr(persisted.threads).length)return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(persisted));const frame=latest?.resultContext?.frame||{},contexts=arr(frame?.contexts),items=[];
  const goalsByContext=new Map();
  for(const t of arr(history).slice(-16)){
    const sd=t?.sovereignDecision||t?.meta?.sovereignDecision||t?.resultContext?.sovereignDecision||{};
    const tid=trim(sd?.thread_mutation?.target_thread_id);if(tid&&trim(sd?.user_goal))goalsByContext.set(tid,trim(sd.user_goal));
  }
  for(const c of contexts.slice(-8)){
    const tid=`TH_${v5ContextHash(trim(c?.context_id)||v5ContextKey(c)).slice(0,8)}`,snap=v5ActiveSnapshot(c),ops=arr(snap?.operations),evidence=ops.map(o=>`EV_${trim(o?.operation_id)}`).filter(x=>x!=='EV_');
    items.push({id:tid,status:trim(c?.context_id)===trim(frame?.active_context_id)?'ACTIVE':'SUSPENDED',goal:goalsByContext.get(tid)||'',entities:[trim(c?.event),...arr(c?.events).map(trim),trim(c?.person||c?.responsible||c?.donor)].filter(Boolean).slice(0,8),dataset_evidence_id:evidence[evidence.length-1]||'',current_view:trim(snap?.presentation?.chart_type)||(snap?.presentation?.chart===true?'chart':'text'),evidence_ids:evidence.slice(-8),last_operation:trim(ops[ops.length-1]?.operation),last_response_mode:'',last_user_goal:'',pending_state:''});
  }
  if(!items.length){const screen=trim(v26EventById(state,selectedEventId)?.titulo);items.push({id:'TH_MAIN',status:'ACTIVE',goal:'',entities:screen?[screen]:[],dataset_evidence_id:'',current_view:'',evidence_ids:[],last_operation:'',last_response_mode:'',last_user_goal:'',pending_state:''});}
  const active=items.find(x=>x.status==='ACTIVE')||items[items.length-1];return{active_thread_id:active?.id||'TH_MAIN',threads:items};
}
function v53RecentDialogue(history=[]){return arr(history).slice(-3).map((x,i)=>({turn:i+1,user:trim(x?.user).slice(0,360),assistant:trim(x?.assistant).slice(0,520)}));}
function v532CompactEntityIndex(state={}){
  const events=arr(state?.eventos).map(x=>[trim(x?.id),trim(x?.titulo||x?.nombre)]).filter(x=>x[0]&&x[1]).slice(0,60);
  const people=arr(state?.personas).map(x=>[trim(x?.id),trim(x?.nombre)]).filter(x=>x[0]&&x[1]).slice(0,180);
  return{format:'[id,name]',events,people};
}
function v53DecorateContext(rc={},history=[],state={},selectedEventId='',plan={},conversationLanguage='es-ES'){const out={...(rc||{})};out.sovereignDecision=v5P15CloneDecisionPlan(plan);out.thread_graph=v53ApplyThreadMutation(v53ThreadGraph(history,state,selectedEventId),plan,out);out.conversation_language=trim(conversationLanguage)||'es-ES';out.ontology_version='CE-ONTOLOGY-1.0';return out;}

function v53ApplyThreadMutation(graph={},plan={},resultContext={}){
  const g=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(graph&&typeof graph==='object'?graph:{active_thread_id:'TH_MAIN',threads:[]})),items=arr(g.threads);let action=trim(plan?.thread_mutation?.action)||'KEEP_THREAD',target=trim(plan?.thread_mutation?.target_thread_id),active=items.find(x=>trim(x.id)===trim(g.active_thread_id))||items[items.length-1];
  if(action==='CREATE_THREAD'){
    if(!target||items.some(x=>trim(x.id)===target))target=`TH_${v5ContextHash(`${Date.now()}|${trim(plan?.user_goal)}`).slice(0,8)}`;
    items.forEach(x=>{if(x.status==='ACTIVE')x.status='SUSPENDED';});active={id:target,status:'ACTIVE',goal:trim(plan?.thread_mutation?.updated_goal||plan?.user_goal),entities:[],dataset_evidence_id:'',current_view:'',evidence_ids:[],last_operation:'',last_response_mode:'',last_user_goal:'',pending_state:''};items.push(active);g.active_thread_id=target;
  }else if(['SWITCH_THREAD','RESUME_THREAD'].includes(action)&&target){const found=items.find(x=>trim(x.id)===target);if(found){items.forEach(x=>{if(x.status==='ACTIVE')x.status='SUSPENDED';});found.status='ACTIVE';active=found;g.active_thread_id=found.id;}}
  else if(action==='SUSPEND_THREAD'){if(active)active.status='SUSPENDED';}
  else if(target){const found=items.find(x=>trim(x.id)===target);if(found)active=found;}
  if(!active){active={id:target||'TH_MAIN',status:'ACTIVE',goal:'',entities:[],dataset_evidence_id:'',current_view:'',evidence_ids:[],last_operation:'',last_response_mode:'',last_user_goal:'',pending_state:''};items.push(active);g.active_thread_id=active.id;}
  if(trim(plan?.thread_mutation?.updated_goal||plan?.user_goal))active.goal=trim(plan?.thread_mutation?.updated_goal||plan?.user_goal);
  const entities=[trim(resultContext?.event),...arr(resultContext?.events).map(trim),trim(resultContext?.person||resultContext?.responsible||resultContext?.donor)].filter(Boolean);if(entities.length)active.entities=[...new Set(entities)].slice(0,10);
  const frame=resultContext?.frame||{},snap=v5ActiveSnapshot(frame),evs=arr(snap?.operations).map(o=>`EV_${trim(o?.operation_id)}`).filter(x=>x!=='EV_');if(evs.length){active.evidence_ids=[...new Set([...(active.evidence_ids||[]),...evs])].slice(-12);active.dataset_evidence_id=evs[evs.length-1];}
  if(trim(resultContext?.chart_type))active.current_view=trim(resultContext.chart_type);else if(resultContext?.chart===true)active.current_view='chart';
  if(trim(resultContext?.operation))active.last_operation=trim(resultContext.operation);active.last_response_mode=trim(plan?.response_goal?.mode)||active.last_response_mode||'';active.last_user_goal=trim(plan?.user_goal)||active.last_user_goal||'';active.pending_state=trim(plan?.state_transition?.kind)||active.pending_state||'';g.threads=items.slice(-12);return g;
}

function v5ContextDomainFromOperation(op=''){
  op=trim(op);
  if(op==='event_weather')return'weather';
  if(op==='event_bank')return'bank';
  if(op==='event_income_status')return'income';
  if(op==='event_purchases')return'purchases';
  if(op==='event_donations')return'donations';
  if(op==='event_attendance')return'attendance';
  if(op==='compare_events')return'comparison';
  if(op==='event_summary')return'summary';
  if(op==='event_documentation')return'documentation';
  if(op==='event_management')return'management';
  if(op==='event_liquidations')return'liquidations';
  if(op==='events_overview')return'events_overview';
  if(op==='person_profile'||op==='person_events')return'person';
  if(op==='recall_memory')return'memory';
  return op||'general';
}

// ============================================================================
// V5 CORE P3 · C/S/O/R/V
// Una sesión contiene varios Contextos lógicos (C). Cada C conserva varios Snapshots
// inmutables (S). Cada S contiene una o más Operaciones ejecutadas (O), los bloques de
// respuesta que dependen de ellas (R) y las vistas/presentaciones (V).
// NHC: esta jerarquía no contiene nombres de personas, eventos, importes ni frases ITV.
// ============================================================================
function v5ContextHash(value=''){
  // Hash técnico estable para IDs internos C/S/O. No contiene semántica de negocio ni datos hard-codeados.
  return Number(v61Hash(String(value??''))).toString(16).padStart(8,'0');
}
function v5ContextKey(frame={}){
  const domain=trim(frame.domain)||v5ContextDomainFromOperation(frame.operation),events=arr(frame.events).map(trim).filter(Boolean),event=trim(frame.event),person=trim(frame.person||frame.responsible||frame.donor);
  const scope=trim(frame.scope),subject=scope==='all_events'?'all_events':(events.length?events.map(norm).sort().join('+'):norm(event||person||'global'));
  return`${domain}:${subject||'global'}`;
}
function v5OperationArgsForSignature(args={}){
  const out={};for(const k of Object.keys(args||{}).sort()){if(['_user_prompt','chart','chart_type'].includes(k))continue;const v=args[k];if(v===undefined||v===null||v==='')continue;out[k]=Array.isArray(v)?v.map(x=>typeof x==='string'?trim(x):x):v;}return out;
}
function v5OperationSignature(args={}){return`${trim(args.operation)}::${JSON.stringify(v5OperationArgsForSignature(args))}`;}
function v5MakeOperation(args={},seq=0,idx=0,sourceOperationId=''){
  const clean={...args};delete clean._user_prompt;
  return{operation_id:`o_${seq}_${idx+1}_${v5ContextHash(v5OperationSignature(clean)).slice(0,7)}`,operation:trim(clean.operation),args:clean,signature:v5OperationSignature(clean),source_operation_id:trim(sourceOperationId),executed:true};
}
function v5ResponseBlocksFromOperations(ops=[]){
  const groups=new Map();for(const o of arr(ops)){const d=v5ContextDomainFromOperation(o.operation),key=d==='purchases'?'purchases':d;if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);groups.get(key).push(o.operation_id);}
  let i=0;return[...groups.entries()].map(([kind,source])=>({response_id:`r_${++i}`,kind,source_operation_ids:source,written:'',spoken:''}));
}
function v5ViewFromOperations(ops=[],chart=false,chartType=''){
  const source=arr(ops).map(o=>o.operation_id);return[{view_id:'v_text',type:'text',source_operation_ids:source},...(chart?[{view_id:`v_${trim(chartType)||'chart'}`,type:trim(chartType)||'bar',source_operation_ids:source}]:[])];
}
function v5LegacySnapshotFromContext(c={},seq=0){
  const actions=arr(c?.last_query_actions).map(x=>({...x}));if(!actions.length&&trim(c?.operation))actions.push({operation:trim(c.operation),event:trim(c.event),events:arr(c.events),person:trim(c.person),responsible:trim(c.responsible),purchase_status:trim(c.purchase_status),start_date:trim(c.start_date),end_date:trim(c.end_date),chart:c.chart===true,chart_type:trim(c.chart_type)});
  const ops=actions.filter(a=>trim(a.operation)).map((a,i)=>v5MakeOperation(a,seq||num(c.last_used_seq)||1,i));
  if(!ops.length)return null;
  const chart=actions.some(a=>a.chart===true)||c.chart===true,ct=trim(actions.find(a=>a.chart===true)?.chart_type)||trim(c.chart_type);
  return{snapshot_id:`s_${v5ContextHash(c.context_id||v5ContextKey(c)).slice(0,6)}_${seq||num(c.last_used_seq)||1}_1`,parent_snapshot_id:'',created_seq:seq||num(c.last_used_seq)||1,operations:ops,response_blocks:v5ResponseBlocksFromOperations(ops),views:v5ViewFromOperations(ops,chart,ct),presentation:{chart,chart_type:ct},event:trim(c.event),events:arr(c.events),person:trim(c.person||c.responsible),responsible:trim(c.responsible||c.person),donor:trim(c.donor),domain:trim(c.domain)||v5ContextDomainFromOperation(c.operation),answer_shape:trim(c.last_answer_shape)};
}
function v5EnsureContextShape(c={},screen='',seq=0){
  const out={...v5BlankFrame(screen),...c,contexts:undefined,snapshots:arr(c?.snapshots).map(x=>({...x,operations:arr(x?.operations).map(o=>({...o,args:{...(o?.args||{})}})),response_blocks:arr(x?.response_blocks).map(r=>({...r,source_operation_ids:arr(r?.source_operation_ids)})),views:arr(x?.views).map(v=>({...v,source_operation_ids:arr(v?.source_operation_ids)}))}))};
  if(!out.context_id)out.context_id=v5ContextKey(out);
  if(!out.snapshots.length){const legacy=v5LegacySnapshotFromContext(out,seq||num(out.last_used_seq));if(legacy){out.snapshots=[legacy];out.active_snapshot_id=legacy.snapshot_id;}}
  if(!trim(out.active_snapshot_id)&&out.snapshots.length)out.active_snapshot_id=trim(out.snapshots[out.snapshots.length-1].snapshot_id);
  return out;
}
function v5ActiveSnapshot(c={}){const ss=arr(c?.snapshots);return ss.find(x=>trim(x.snapshot_id)===trim(c?.active_snapshot_id))||ss[ss.length-1]||null;}
function v5ProjectSnapshotToLegacy(c={}){
  const s=v5ActiveSnapshot(c);if(!s)return c;const actions=arr(s.operations).map(o=>({...o.args})),primary=actions[0]||{};
  return{...c,event:trim(s.event)||trim(primary.event)||trim(c.event),events:arr(s.events).length?arr(s.events):arr(primary.events||c.events),person:trim(s.person),responsible:trim(s.responsible||s.person),donor:trim(s.donor),operation:trim(primary.operation)||trim(c.operation),last_query_actions:actions,last_data_operation:actions.length>1?'compound':trim(primary.operation)||trim(c.last_data_operation),last_data_event:trim(primary.event)||trim(s.event)||trim(c.last_data_event),last_data_events:arr(primary.events).length?arr(primary.events):arr(c.last_data_events),last_data_start_date:trim(primary.start_date)||trim(c.last_data_start_date),last_data_end_date:trim(primary.end_date)||trim(primary.start_date)||trim(c.last_data_end_date),start_date:trim(primary.start_date)||trim(c.start_date),end_date:trim(primary.end_date)||trim(primary.start_date)||trim(c.end_date),chart:s.presentation?.chart===true,chart_type:trim(s.presentation?.chart_type)||trim(c.chart_type),last_answer_shape:trim(s.answer_shape)||trim(c.last_answer_shape)};
}
function v5BlankFrame(screen=''){
  return{screen_event:screen,context_id:'',domain:'',event:'',events:[],person:'',responsible:'',donor:'',operation:'',purchase_status:'',table_key:'',weather_date:'',start_date:'',end_date:'',chart:false,chart_type:'',report_mode:'',report_audience:'',report_event:'',last_query_actions:[],last_data_operation:'',last_data_event:'',last_data_events:[],last_data_start_date:'',last_data_end_date:'',last_presentation_kind:'',last_answer_shape:'',last_used_seq:0,snapshots:[],active_snapshot_id:'',contexts:[],active_context_id:'',route_domain:''};
}
function v5ConversationBookFromHistory(history=[],state={},selectedEventId=''){
  const screen=trim(v26EventById(state,selectedEventId)?.titulo||v26EventById(state,selectedEventId)?.nombre||''),byId=new Map();let active='',seq=0;
  const put=(raw)=>{const c=v5EnsureContextShape(raw,screen,Math.max(1,num(raw?.last_used_seq)||seq));if(trim(c.context_id)){const prev=byId.get(c.context_id);if(prev){const mergedSnaps=[...arr(prev.snapshots),...arr(c.snapshots)].reduce((acc,x)=>{if(!acc.some(y=>trim(y.snapshot_id)===trim(x.snapshot_id)))acc.push(x);return acc;},[]).sort((a,b)=>num(a.created_seq)-num(b.created_seq)).slice(-6);byId.set(c.context_id,{...prev,...c,snapshots:mergedSnaps,active_snapshot_id:trim(c.active_snapshot_id)||trim(prev.active_snapshot_id)});}else byId.set(c.context_id,c);}};
  for(const turn of arr(history)){
    const rc=turn?.resultContext;if(!rc||typeof rc!=='object')continue;const src=(rc.frame&&typeof rc.frame==='object')?rc.frame:rc;seq++;
    if(arr(src?.contexts).length){for(const c0 of arr(src.contexts))put(c0);if(trim(src.active_context_id))active=trim(src.active_context_id);continue;}
    const legacy=v5EnsureContextShape({...src,domain:trim(src.domain)||v5ContextDomainFromOperation(src.operation),context_id:trim(src.context_id)||v5ContextKey(src),last_used_seq:seq},screen,seq);if(legacy.operation||legacy.last_query_actions.length){put(legacy);active=legacy.context_id;}
  }
  const contexts=[...byId.values()].map(v5ProjectSnapshotToLegacy).sort((a,b)=>num(a.last_used_seq)-num(b.last_used_seq)).slice(-12);if(!contexts.some(c=>c.context_id===active))active=trim(contexts[contexts.length-1]?.context_id);
  return{screen_event:screen,active_context_id:active,contexts,seq:Math.max(seq,...contexts.map(c=>num(c.last_used_seq)))};
}
function v5PromptRouteSignals(userPrompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(userPrompt),sem=v4BrainLitePromptSemantics(userPrompt),domains=[];
  const add=d=>{if(d&&!domains.includes(d))domains.push(d);};
  if(/\b(?:recuerd\w*|conversacion\w*\s+(?:anterior|pasad\w*)|hablamos?\s+(?:antes|hace))\b/.test(p))add('memory');
  // P5 · taxonomía explícita de dominio. Son conceptos funcionales de ControlEvent, no frases ITV.
  // Un objeto factual escrito por el usuario manda sobre la presentación y sobre el C activo.
  if(sem.explicitWeather||/\b(?:tiempo|meteorolog\w*|prevision|temperatur\w*|lluvia)\b/.test(p))add('weather');
  if(sem.explicitCompare||/\bcomparaci\w*|comparar|compara\b/.test(p))add('comparison');
  if(sem.bank||/\b(?:banco|bancari\w*|cuadre|abonos?|cargos?|flujo\s+neto|saldo\s+calculad\w*)\b/.test(p))add('bank');
  if(sem.donation||/\b(?:donacion\w*|donante\w*|donad\w*)\b/.test(p))add('donations');
  if(sem.attendanceAll||/\basistenc\w*\b/.test(p))add('attendance');
  if(sem.purchase||sem.responsibleBreakdown||sem.topResponsible||/\b(?:compras?|comprad\w*|gastad\w*|responsables?\s+de\s+compras?)\b/.test(p))add('purchases');
  if(sem.attendeeIncome||sem.income)add('income');if(sem.summary)add('summary');
  const presentation=sem.chartFollow||/\b(?:barras?|lineas?|líneas?|donut|queso|tarta|graf\w*)\b/.test(p),reopen=/\b(?:vuelve|volvamos|retoma|retomemos|antes|aquell\w*|lo\s+de|por\s+donde\s+iba|por\s+dónde\s+iba)\b/.test(p);
  // Ancla conversacional: «vuelve/retoma/al resumen/al banco/a las compras...». Es taxonomía
  // de dominios, no frases de caso ni entidades. Si existe, manda sobre menciones accesorias.
  const anchors=[
    ['memory',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|al|a\s+la)\b.{0,24}\b(?:recuerdo|conversacion\s+anterior)\b/],
    ['comparison',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|a\s+la|la\s+misma)\b.{0,24}\bcomparaci\w*\b/],
    ['summary',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|al|a\s+ese)\b.{0,24}\bresumen\b|\b(?:añade|suma|incluye)\b.{0,40}\bal\s+resumen\b/],
    ['bank',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|al)\b.{0,24}\b(?:banco|cuadre|saldo)\b/],
    ['weather',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|al)\b.{0,24}\b(?:tiempo|meteorolog|prevision)\b/],
    ['purchases',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|a\s+las?)\b.{0,24}\b(?:compras?|responsables?)\b/],
    ['donations',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|a\s+las?)\b.{0,24}\bdonacion\w*\b/],
    ['income',/\b(?:vuelve|retoma|volvamos|a\s+los?)\b.{0,24}\bingres\w*\b/]
  ];
  let anchor_domain='';for(const [d,re] of anchors)if(re.test(p)){anchor_domain=d;break;}
  return{domain:anchor_domain||domains[0]||'',domains,anchor_domain,presentation,reopen,chart_type:sem.chartType||''};
}
function v5SelectContextFromBook(book={}){
  const list=arr(book.contexts),active=list.find(c=>trim(c.context_id)===trim(book.active_context_id))||null;
  // V5.2 CLEAN SOVEREIGN: CE no lee el lenguaje del usuario para elegir contexto.
  // Solo expone el contexto certificado activo (o el último certificado si falta el puntero).
  return active||list[list.length-1]||null;
}
function v5ConversationFrameFromHistory(history=[],state={},selectedEventId=''){
  const book=v5ConversationBookFromHistory(history,state,selectedEventId),chosen=v5SelectContextFromBook(book),frame={...v5BlankFrame(book.screen_event),...(chosen||{})};
  frame.screen_event=book.screen_event;frame.contexts=book.contexts.map(c=>({...c,contexts:undefined}));frame.active_context_id=trim(chosen?.context_id||book.active_context_id);return v5ProjectSnapshotToLegacy(frame);
}
function v4BrainLiteContext(history=[],state={},selectedEventId='',userPrompt=''){
  const f=v5ConversationFrameFromHistory(history,state,selectedEventId);
  return{...f,event:trim(f.scope)==='all_events'?'':(trim(f.event)||trim(f.screen_event)),scope:trim(f.scope),events:arr(f.events),person:trim(f.person||f.responsible),responsible:trim(f.responsible||f.person),donor:trim(f.donor)};
}
function v5ContinuationAddsOperations(userPrompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(userPrompt);return /^\s*(?:y\b|ademas\b|además\b|tambien\b|también\b|añade\b|anade\b|suma\b|incluye\b)/.test(p)||/\b(?:tambien|también|ademas|además|junto\s+con|ademas\s+de|además\s+de)\b/.test(p);
}
// V5 P6 · firma semántica de una S. No contiene frases ITV ni entidades concretas: deriva
// exclusivamente de los dominios funcionales que el usuario pide en el turno.
function v5ExpectedOperationSetFromPrompt(userPrompt=''){
  const sem=v4BrainLitePromptSemantics(userPrompt),out=[];const add=op=>{if(op&&!out.includes(op))out.push(op);};
  if(sem.summary)add('event_summary');if(sem.bank)add('event_bank');if(sem.income&&(/\bingres\w*\b|\bpendiente\s+de\s+ingreso\b|\bbizum\b|\bpagad\w*\b|\baportad\w*\b/.test(vnextP17LooseNorm(userPrompt))))add('event_income_status');
  if(sem.purchase)add('event_purchases');if(sem.donation)add('event_donations');if(sem.explicitWeather)add('event_weather');if(sem.explicitCompare)add('compare_events');if(sem.attendanceAll)add('event_attendance');
  return out;
}
function v5BestSnapshotForOperations(frame={},wantedOps=[]){
  const wanted=[...new Set(arr(wantedOps).map(trim).filter(Boolean))];if(!wanted.length)return null;const ss=arr(frame?.snapshots);let best=null,bestScore=-1e9;
  for(const s of ss){const ops=arr(s?.operations).map(o=>trim(o?.operation||o?.args?.operation)).filter(Boolean),set=new Set(ops);if(!wanted.every(op=>set.has(op)))continue;let score=wanted.length*100-Math.max(0,ops.length-wanted.length)*8+num(s?.created_seq);
    if(wanted.includes('event_purchases')){const pst=new Set(arr(s?.operations).filter(o=>trim(o?.operation)==='event_purchases').map(o=>trim(o?.args?.purchase_status)).filter(Boolean));const sem=v4BrainLitePromptSemantics(frame?._route_prompt||'');if(sem.pending&&sem.realized&&pst.has('pending')&&pst.has('realized'))score+=40;}
    if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;best=s;}
  }return best;
}
function v5CloneOperation(o={}){return{...o,args:{...(o?.args||{})},evidence:o?.evidence?{...o.evidence,tables:arr(o.evidence.tables).map(t=>({...t,rows:arr(t.rows).map(r=>({...r}))})),charts:arr(o.evidence.charts).map(c=>({...c,labels:arr(c.labels),values:arr(c.values),series:arr(c.series).map(x=>({...x,values:arr(x.values)}))}))}:undefined};}
function v5MergeOperationsImmutable(prevOps=[],newArgs=[],evidenceBySig=new Map(),seq=0){
  const merged=arr(prevOps).map(v5CloneOperation);for(const a0 of arr(newArgs)){const a={...a0},sig=v5OperationSignature(a);let same=merged.find(o=>trim(o.signature)===sig);if(!same){same=v5MakeOperation(a,seq,merged.length);merged.push(same);}else{same.args={...same.args,...a};same.signature=v5OperationSignature(same.args);}if(evidenceBySig.has(sig))same.evidence=evidenceBySig.get(sig);}
  return merged.slice(-8);
}
function v5CompactEvidence(result={}){
  // V5.2 · compactación mecánica de evidencia YA CERTIFICADA. No lee lenguaje humano,
  // no decide dominio ni intención y nunca participa en la elección de acciones.
  const keep=new Set(['weather','by_responsible','by_product','donors','by_donor','economics_chart','income_status','income_status_all_events','comparison','event_comparison','summary_by_event','income_by_event','kpis','hitos','tasks']);
  let picked=arr(result?.tables).filter(t=>keep.has(trim(t?.key)));
  if(!picked.length&&/memory|recall/i.test(`${trim(result?.name)} ${trim(result?.facts?.action)} ${trim(result?.title)}`))picked=arr(result?.tables).slice(0,3);
  const tables=picked.map(t=>({key:trim(t.key),title:trim(t.title),rows:arr(t.rows).slice(0,40).map(r=>({...r}))}));
  return{facts:{...(result?.facts||{})},tables,charts:arr(result?.charts).slice(0,4).map(c=>({...c,labels:arr(c.labels).slice(0,40),values:arr(c.values).slice(0,40),series:arr(c.series).slice(0,6).map(x=>({...x,values:arr(x.values).slice(0,40)}))}))};
}

function v5FrameAfterTurn(prev={},plan={},good=[],resultContext={}){
  const executed=good.map(x=>({...x.args})),primary=executed[0]||{},bookContexts=arr(prev?.contexts).map(c=>v5EnsureContextShape({...c,contexts:undefined},trim(prev?.screen_event),num(c?.last_used_seq))),book=new Map(bookContexts.filter(c=>trim(c.context_id)).map(c=>[trim(c.context_id),c]));
  const base=v5EnsureContextShape({...v5BlankFrame(trim(prev?.screen_event)),...prev,contexts:undefined},trim(prev?.screen_event),num(prev?.last_used_seq));
  const primaryDomain=v5ContextDomainFromOperation(trim(primary.operation)||trim(resultContext?.operation)||trim(base.operation));
  const domain=primaryDomain||trim(resultContext?.domain)||trim(base.domain);
  let subject={...base,domain};
  if(trim(base.domain)&&trim(base.domain)!==trim(domain)){subject.person='';subject.responsible='';subject.donor='';}
  if(trim(primary.scope)||trim(resultContext?.scope))subject.scope=trim(primary.scope||resultContext.scope);
  if(trim(subject.scope)==='all_events')subject.event='';else if(trim(primary.event))subject.event=trim(primary.event);
  if(arr(primary.events).length)subject.events=arr(primary.events).map(trim).filter(Boolean);
  if(trim(resultContext?.person||resultContext?.responsible)){subject.person=trim(resultContext.person||resultContext.responsible);subject.responsible=trim(resultContext.responsible||resultContext.person);}
  if(trim(resultContext?.donor))subject.donor=trim(resultContext.donor);
  if(executed.some(a=>trim(a?.operation)==='event_purchases'&&trim(a?.group_by)==='responsible')){subject.person='';subject.responsible='';}
  subject.operation=trim(primary.operation)||trim(resultContext?.operation)||subject.operation;
  subject.purchase_status=trim(resultContext?.purchase_status)||trim(primary.purchase_status)||subject.purchase_status;
  subject.context_id=v5ContextKey(subject);
  let target=book.get(subject.context_id);
  if(!target&&trim(prev?.active_context_id)&&book.has(trim(prev.active_context_id))&&trim(book.get(trim(prev.active_context_id))?.domain)===domain)target=book.get(trim(prev.active_context_id));
  if(!target)target=v5EnsureContextShape({...subject,context_id:subject.context_id,snapshots:[],active_snapshot_id:''},trim(prev?.screen_event),0);
  const previousSnapshot=v5ActiveSnapshot(target),seq=Math.max(num(prev?.last_used_seq),...bookContexts.map(c=>num(c.last_used_seq)))+1;
  const evidenceBySig=new Map(good.map(x=>[v5OperationSignature(x.args),v5CompactEvidence(x.result)]));
  const operations=executed.slice(0,8).map((a,i)=>{const old=arr(previousSnapshot?.operations).find(o=>o.signature===v5OperationSignature(a)),o=v5MakeOperation(a,seq,i,old?.operation_id||'');o.evidence=evidenceBySig.get(o.signature);return o;});
  const chart=executed.some(a=>a.chart===true)||resultContext?.chart===true;
  const chartType=trim(executed.find(a=>a.chart===true)?.chart_type)||trim(resultContext?.chart_type)||trim(previousSnapshot?.presentation?.chart_type);
  const snapshot={snapshot_id:`s_${v5ContextHash(target.context_id||subject.context_id).slice(0,6)}_${seq}_${arr(target.snapshots).length+1}`,parent_snapshot_id:trim(previousSnapshot?.snapshot_id),created_seq:seq,domain,event:trim(subject.event),events:arr(subject.events),person:trim(subject.person),responsible:trim(subject.responsible),donor:trim(subject.donor),operations,response_blocks:v5ResponseBlocksFromOperations(operations),views:v5ViewFromOperations(operations,chart,chartType),presentation:{chart,chart_type:chartType},answer_shape:chart?'visual':'data'};
  const snapshots=[...arr(target.snapshots),snapshot].sort((a,b)=>num(a.created_seq)-num(b.created_seq)).slice(-6);
  target={...target,...subject,domain,context_id:target.context_id||subject.context_id,snapshots,active_snapshot_id:snapshot.snapshot_id,last_used_seq:seq,chart,chart_type:chartType,last_query_actions:operations.map(o=>({...o.args})),last_data_operation:operations.length>1?'compound':trim(operations[0]?.operation),last_data_event:trim(operations[0]?.args?.event)||trim(subject.event),last_data_events:arr(operations[0]?.args?.events),last_data_start_date:trim(operations[0]?.args?.start_date),last_data_end_date:trim(operations[0]?.args?.end_date)||trim(operations[0]?.args?.start_date)};
  book.set(target.context_id,target);
  const contexts=[...book.values()].sort((a,b)=>num(a.last_used_seq)-num(b.last_used_seq)).slice(-12);
  return{...v5ProjectSnapshotToLegacy(target),contexts,active_context_id:target.context_id,screen_event:trim(prev?.screen_event),last_used_seq:seq};
}
function v5AttachResponseAndViews(frame={},written='',spoken='',charts=[],tables=[]){
  const contexts=arr(frame?.contexts).map(c=>v5EnsureContextShape({...c,contexts:undefined},trim(frame?.screen_event),num(c?.last_used_seq))),idx=contexts.findIndex(c=>trim(c.context_id)===trim(frame?.active_context_id));if(idx<0)return frame;
  const c=contexts[idx],ss=arr(c.snapshots),si=ss.findIndex(x=>trim(x.snapshot_id)===trim(c.active_snapshot_id));if(si<0)return frame;const snap={...ss[si]},ops=arr(snap.operations),blocks=arr(snap.response_blocks).map((r,i)=>({...r,written:i===0?trim(written).slice(0,1200):'',spoken:i===0?trim(spoken).slice(0,800):''}));
  const source=ops.map(o=>o.operation_id),views=[{view_id:'v_text',type:'text',source_operation_ids:source}];if(arr(tables).length)views.push({view_id:'v_table',type:'table',source_operation_ids:source,count:arr(tables).length});for(let i=0;i<arr(charts).length;i++)views.push({view_id:`v_chart_${i+1}`,type:trim(charts[i]?.type)||'chart',source_operation_ids:source,title:trim(charts[i]?.title)});
  ss[si]={...snap,response_blocks:blocks,views};contexts[idx]={...c,snapshots:ss};return{...frame,contexts,active_snapshot_id:ss[si].snapshot_id};
}

// ============================================================================
// V5 CORE P4 · SOBERANIA C/S + VIEW ENGINE + CONTEXT PAGING
// C decide el dominio. S fija el estado semántico. O solo aporta evidencia. V solo presenta.
// El planner recibe únicamente el C/S relevante y un índice compacto del resto.
// ============================================================================
function v5CompactPlannerOperation(o={}){
  const a={...(o?.args||{})};delete a._user_prompt;delete a.view_filters;delete a.hidden_columns;delete a.visible_columns;
  return{operation:trim(o?.operation||a.operation),args:a};
}
function v5CompactPlannerContext(c={}){
  const s=v5ActiveSnapshot(c),ops=arr(s?.operations).map(v5CompactPlannerOperation).slice(0,8);
  return{context_id:trim(c.context_id),domain:trim(c.domain),event:trim(c.event),events:arr(c.events).map(trim).filter(Boolean).slice(0,4),person:trim(c.person||c.responsible),responsible:trim(c.responsible||c.person),donor:trim(c.donor),active_snapshot_id:trim(c.active_snapshot_id),snapshot:{domain:trim(s?.domain||c.domain),event:trim(s?.event||c.event),events:arr(s?.events).map(trim).filter(Boolean).slice(0,4),person:trim(s?.person),operations:ops,presentation:{chart:s?.presentation?.chart===true,chart_type:trim(s?.presentation?.chart_type)},answer_shape:trim(s?.answer_shape)},last_used_seq:num(c.last_used_seq)};
}
function v5PlannerContextPage(history=[],state={},selectedEventId=''){
  const book=v5ConversationBookFromHistory(history,state,selectedEventId),active=v5SelectContextFromBook(book);
  // V5.2 CLEAN SOVEREIGN: this page is an inert fact book. No prompt routing, no scoring,
  // no linguistic selection. Gemini receives recent certified contexts and decides relevance.
  return {
    screen_event:book.screen_event,
    active_context_id:trim(book.active_context_id),
    active_context:active?{...v5ProjectSnapshotToLegacy(active),contexts:undefined}:null,
    contexts:book.contexts.slice(-12).map(c=>({...v5ProjectSnapshotToLegacy(c),contexts:undefined}))
  };
}
function v5P10MaskResolvedEntityLabels(prompt='',labels=[]){
  let p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);
  // P10 · Las palabras que forman parte de una ENTIDAD YA RESUELTA (sobre todo el nombre
  // de un evento) no pueden convertirse en intención. Ej.: «gastos» dentro del título de
  // un evento no significa por sí mismo COMPRAS. Es aislamiento léxico de entidad, NHC.
  for(const raw of arr(labels)){const n=vnextP17LooseNorm(raw);if(n&&n.length>=3)p=p.split(n).join(' ');}
  return p.replace(/\s{2,}/g,' ').trim();
}
function v5P9FactualContract(prompt='',activeCtx={},resolvedLabels=[]){
  const p=v5P10MaskResolvedEntityLabels(prompt,[...arr(resolvedLabels),trim(activeCtx?.event),...arr(activeCtx?.events)]),sem=v4BrainLitePromptSemantics(p),additive=v5ContinuationAddsOperations(prompt),groupResponsible=/\bresponsabl\w*\b/.test(p)&&(sem.responsibleBreakdown||/\b(?:por|segun|según)\s+responsabl\w*\b/.test(p));
  const explicit={
    comparison:sem.explicitCompare||/\b(?:comparar|compara|comparativa)\b/.test(p),
    purchases:sem.purchase||groupResponsible||/\bcompras?\b/.test(p),
    donations:sem.donation||/\bdonaci\w*|donante\w*\b/.test(p),
    bank:sem.bank||/\b(?:banco|bancari\w*|cuadre|flujo\s+neto|saldo\s+calculad\w*)\b/.test(p),
    income:sem.attendeeIncome||sem.income||/\b(?:pendiente\s+de\s+ingreso|bizum|efectivo|ingres\w*)\b/.test(p),
    weather:sem.explicitWeather,
    attendance:sem.attendanceAll||/\basistenc\w*\b/.test(p),
    summary:sem.summary
  };
  let domain='';
  if(v5SynthesisCue(prompt))domain='synthesis';
  // «resumen económico/general/del evento» es intención primaria aunque el nombre de la
  // entidad o el propio resumen cite compras/ingresos. No se hard-codean eventos concretos.
  else if(explicit.summary&&/\bresumen\b/.test(p)&&!/\bresumen\s+(?:de|sobre)\s+compras?\b/.test(p))domain='summary';
  else for(const d of ['comparison','purchases','donations','bank','income','weather','attendance','summary'])if(explicit[d]){domain=d;break;}
  // Una continuación aditiva amplía el C activo si el nuevo concepto es una faceta de ese C.
  if(additive&&trim(activeCtx?.domain)==='bank'&&domain==='income')domain='bank';
  if(additive&&trim(activeCtx?.domain)==='summary'&&['bank','income'].includes(domain))domain='summary';
  const priorResponsible=trim(activeCtx?.domain)==='purchases'&&arr(activeCtx?.last_query_actions).some(a=>trim(a?.operation)==='event_purchases'&&trim(a?.group_by)==='responsible');
  const inheritResponsible=priorResponsible&&!groupResponsible&&!/\b(?:producto|tienda|segmento|destino|fecha)\b/.test(p)&&/\b(?:agrup\w*|orden\w*|ranking|lista|mayor|menor|importe|gastad\w*)\b/.test(p);
  // Una continuación elíptica de orden/lista sobre un S de compras por responsable sigue
  // siendo PURCHASES aunque no repita la palabra «compras».
  if(!domain&&inheritResponsible)domain='purchases';
  return{domain,additive,group_by:(groupResponsible||inheritResponsible)?'responsible':'',sort_amount_desc:/\b(?:mayor\s+a\s+menor|descendente|decreciente)\b/.test(p),view_requested:sem.chartFollow,view_type:sem.chartType||'',view_capability:v5P9ViewCapabilityCue(prompt),unsupported_view:v5P9UnsupportedViewCue(prompt)};
}

function v5P10SelfPersonCue(prompt=''){const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);return /^(?:y\s+)?yo\b|\b(?:sobre|de)\s+mi\b/.test(p);}
function v5P10MemoryRefreshCue(prompt=''){const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);return /\b(?:actualiz\w*|datos\s+actuales|ahora\s+ya|repite\w*.{0,35}actualiz\w*)\b/.test(p);}
function v5P10GeneralPersonCue(prompt=''){const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);return /\b(?:info(?:rmacion)?\s+general|cuentame|cuéntame|que\s+fue\s+de|qué\s+fue\s+de)\b/.test(p)||/^\s*y\s+[^?]{1,60}\??\s*$/.test(p);}
function v5P10ForgetEntityCue(prompt=''){return /\b(?:olvida|olvidate|olvídate|ignora|sin\s+tener\s+en\s+cuenta|deja\s+fuera)\b/.test(vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt));}

// ============================================================================
// V5 CORE P11 · MEMORY MODE + SUBJECT/EVENT REFINEMENTS
// Presentación de recuerdos, comparación entonces/ahora y pequeñas reglas de
// autoridad semántica basadas en catálogos reales. Sin nombres/importes de negocio.

function v5P11DateFromAny(value=''){
  const raw=trim(value);if(!raw)return null;
  let d=new Date(raw);if(Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))return d;
  const m=raw.match(/\b(\d{1,2})[\/.-](\d{1,2})[\/.-](20\d{2})\b/);if(m){d=new Date(Number(m[3]),Number(m[2])-1,Number(m[1]));if(Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))return d;}
  return null;
}
function v5P11MemoryDateRaw(result={}){
  const f=result?.facts||{},candidates=[f.started_at,f.start_date,f.created_at,f.matched_created_at,f.ended_at];
  for(const k of ['memory_turns','memory_evidence','memory_matches'])for(const r of arr(v4BrainLiteTable(result,k)?.rows))for(const key of ['Fecha','fecha','created_at','Creado','Inicio'])candidates.push(r?.[key]);
  for(const v of candidates){if(v5P11DateFromAny(v))return trim(v);}
  return'';
}
function v5P11MemoryAgeText(result={},nowIso=''){
  const d=v5P11DateFromAny(v5P11MemoryDateRaw(result)),now=v5P11DateFromAny(nowIso)||new Date();if(!d)return'';
  const days=Math.max(0,Math.floor((now.getTime()-d.getTime())/86400000));
  if(days===0)return'hoy';if(days===1)return'hace 1 día';if(days<14)return`hace ${days} días`;
  if(days<60){const w=Math.round(days/7);return`hace ${w} ${w===1?'semana':'semanas'}`;}
  if(days<730){const m=Math.max(1,Math.round(days/30.44));return`hace ${m} ${m===1?'mes':'meses'}`;}
  const y=Math.max(1,Math.round(days/365.25));return`hace ${y} ${y===1?'año':'años'}`;
}
function v5P11MemoryPresentation(result={},person='',historicalAnswer='',nowIso='',historicalContract=null){
  const ageText=v5P11MemoryAgeText(result,nowIso),occurredAt=v5P11MemoryDateRaw(result),hc=historicalContract&&typeof historicalContract==='object'?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(historicalContract)):null;
  return{active:true,mode:'memory',ageText,occurredAt,subject:trim(person),historicalAnswer:trim(historicalAnswer),historicalContract:hc,canRefresh:!!hc};
}
function v5P11LastMemoryTurn(history=[]){
  for(let i=arr(history).length-1;i>=0;i--){const t=arr(history)[i]||{},rc=t.resultContext||{},m=t.memoryPresentation||t?.meta?.memoryPresentation,mc=t.memoryComparison||t?.meta?.memoryComparison;
    if(mc?.active&&trim(mc.historicalAnswer)){
      const mp={active:true,mode:'memory',ageText:trim(mc.ageText),occurredAt:trim(mc.occurredAt),subject:trim(mc.subject),historicalAnswer:trim(mc.historicalAnswer),canRefresh:!!(mc.historicalContract&&typeof mc.historicalContract==='object'),historicalContract:(mc.historicalContract&&typeof mc.historicalContract==='object')?mc.historicalContract:null};
      return{answer:trim(mc.historicalAnswer),person:trim(mc.subject),event:trim(rc.event),memoryPresentation:mp,resultContext:{...rc,memory_historical_contract:mp.historicalContract||rc.memory_historical_contract||null}};
    }
    if(trim(rc.domain)==='memory'||trim(rc.operation)==='recall_memory'||m?.active){
      const hc=(m?.historicalContract&&typeof m.historicalContract==='object')?m.historicalContract:((rc.memory_historical_contract&&typeof rc.memory_historical_contract==='object')?rc.memory_historical_contract:null),mp=m&&typeof m==='object'?{...m,historicalContract:hc,canRefresh:!!hc}:(hc?{active:true,mode:'memory',ageText:trim(rc.memory_age_text),occurredAt:trim(rc.memory_occurred_at),subject:trim(rc.person),historicalAnswer:trim(t.assistantWritten||t.assistant||''),historicalContract:hc,canRefresh:true}:null);
      return{answer:trim(mp?.historicalAnswer||t.assistantWritten||t.assistant||''),person:trim(rc.person||mp?.subject),event:trim(rc.event),memoryPresentation:mp,resultContext:rc};
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function v5P11MemoryReturnCue(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);return /\b(?:vuelve|vuelva|volver|retoma|retome|regresa|regrese|repite)\b.{0,45}\b(?:recuerdo|respuesta\s+del\s+recuerdo|conversacion\s+recordada)\b/.test(p)||/\b(?:dame|muestra)\b.{0,30}\b(?:otra\s+vez|de\s+nuevo)\b.{0,25}\brecuerdo\b/.test(p)||/^\s*(?:al|a\s+ese|a\+aquel)\s+recuerdo\s*$/.test(p);
}

function v5P11EventGeneralCue(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);return /\b(?:como|cómo|que|qué)\s+(?:ha\s+ido|tal\s+va|tal\s+fue|fue|va)\b|\b(?:info|informacion|información|resumen|estado|situacion|situación)\b/.test(p);

function v5P11ProductFamilyFromCatalog(state={},prompt=''){
  const stop=new Set(['cuanto','cuánto','hemos','gastado','gastos','estos','estas','evento','pantalla','compras','compra','dame','solo','ahora','responsable','responsables','importe','total','estos']);
  const words=(vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt).match(/[a-záéíóúüñ0-9]{4,}/g)||[]).filter(w=>!stop.has(w));
  if(!words.length)return'';
  const products=arr(state?.productos).map(x=>trim(x?.nombre||x?.Nombre||x?.descripcion||x?.Descripción)).filter(Boolean);
  let best='',score=0;
  for(const w of words){let hits=0;for(const name of products){const n=vnextP17LooseNorm(name);if(new RegExp(`(?:^|\\s)${w.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')}(?:\\s|$)`).test(n)||n.includes(w))hits++;}
    if(hits>score&&hits>=2){best=w;score=hits;}
  }
  return best;
}

// V5 P14 · SCOPE AUTHORITY. El alcance es soberano igual que dominio/sujeto.
// No contiene nombres de eventos ni reglas de negocio específicas: sólo interpreta el ámbito pedido.
function v5P14ScopeContract(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);
  const all=/\b(?:todos?|todas?)\s+(?:los\s+)?eventos\b|\b(?:cualquier|cualquiera\s+de\s+los)\s+eventos\b|\ben\s+otros\s+eventos\b|\bnivel\s+(?:mas\s+)?alto\b/.test(p);
  const current=/\b(?:este|ese|el)\s+evento\b|\bevento\s+(?:actual|en\s+pantalla|activo)\b/.test(p);
  return{kind:all?'all_events':current?'current_event':'inherited',explicit:all||current};
}
function v5P14SocialReactionCue(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);
  const factual=/\b(?:ingres\w*|compr\w*|donaci\w*|banco|cuadre|tiempo|meteorolog\w*|asistenc\w*|evento|pendient\w*|graf\w*|persona|responsabl\w*)\b/.test(p);
  if(factual)return false;
  return /\b(?:que\s+fumas|qué\s+fumas|vaya\s+mierda|que\s+locura|qué\s+locura|esto\s+no\s+es\s+serio|te\s+has\s+desviado|te\s+quedas\s+enganchado|te\s+estas\s+liando|te\s+estás\s+liando|te\s+has\s+liado|te\s+lias\s+otra\s+vez|te\s+lías\s+otra\s+vez|que\s+coño|qué\s+coño|y\s+esto\s+que\s+es|y\s+esto\s+qué\s+es)\b/.test(p);
}
function v5P14SocialReactionReply(prompt='',history=[]){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt),last=arr(history).slice(-1)[0],lastOp=trim(last?.resultContext?.operation),lastEvent=trim(last?.resultContext?.event);
  if(/\b(?:y\s+esto\s+que\s+es|y\s+esto\s+qué\s+es)\b/.test(p))return lastOp?`Eso que te acabo de dar pertenece a ${lastOp}${lastEvent?` de ${lastEvent}`:''}. Si no venía a cuento, no lo tomo como nuevo hilo.`:'Esa respuesta no venía a cuento. No la tomo como nuevo hilo.';
  return 'Sí, aquí me he desviado. Mantengo intacto el último contexto válido y no voy a convertir esta reacción en una consulta nueva.';
}
async function v5P14AllEventsIncomeStatus(state={},selectedEventId='',status='pending',population='all',id='p14_income_all'){
  const events=arr(state?.eventos).map(e=>({id:trim(e?.id),name:trim(e?.titulo||e?.nombre||e?.descripcion)})).filter(e=>e.id&&e.name);
  const chunks=[];
  for(let i=0;i<events.length;i+=8){
    const part=events.slice(i,i+8);
    const got=await Promise.all(part.map(async ev=>{try{const base=await v26ToolEventPeople({id:`${id}_${i}`,name:'event_people',event:ev.name,scope:'named_event',detail:'full'},state,selectedEventId),filtered=vnextP1FilteredIncomeResult(base,status,`${id}_${i}`,population),rows=arr(vnextP1Table(filtered,'income_status')?.rows).map(r=>({Evento:ev.name,...r}));return rows;}catch(_){return[];}}));
    chunks.push(...got.flat());
  }
  const total=v26Money(chunks.reduce((a,r)=>a+num(r?.['Importe total']??r?.Importe),0)),matchingEvents=new Set(chunks.map(r=>trim(r?.Evento)).filter(Boolean));
  const schema={Evento:v26TextSchema(),Persona:v26TextSchema(),Número:v26CountSchema('personas'),Rango:v26TextSchema(),'Situación / forma registrada':v26TextSchema(),'Importe total':v26MoneySchema('Importe del ingreso')};
  return{id,name:'query_ce',ok:true,title:`Ingresos ${status==='pending'?'pendientes':'por estado'} · todos los eventos`,facts:{operation:'event_income_status',scope:'all_events',event:'',status,population,record_count:chunks.length,event_count:events.length,matching_event_count:matchingEvents.size,amount_total:total,source_contract:'Todos los eventos registrados; filas de ingresos/colaboradores filtradas por estado. El evento activo no limita el ámbito.'},facts_schema:{record_count:v26CountSchema('registros'),event_count:v26CountSchema('eventos'),matching_event_count:v26CountSchema('eventos'),amount_total:v26MoneySchema('Importe total')},provenance:'ControlEvent · event_people.people · scope all_events',tables:[v26Table('income_status_all_events','Ingresos por estado · todos los eventos',chunks,schema)],_vnext_operation:'event_income_status'};
}
function v5ExplicitFactualDomain(prompt=''){
  const sig=v5PromptRouteSignals(prompt),p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);
  if(!sig.domains.length)return'';
  // Una mención meramente anafórica de presentación no crea dominio factual nuevo.
  if(sig.presentation&&sig.reopen&&sig.anchor_domain)return sig.anchor_domain;
  return sig.domains[0]||'';
}

function v5SynthesisCue(prompt=''){

  const broad=/\b(?:toda|todo|conjunto|global|integral)\b.{0,35}\b(?:info|informacion|datos|evento)\b|\b(?:utilizando|usando)\b.{0,35}\b(?:toda|todo)\b.{0,20}\b(?:info|informacion|datos)\b/.test(p);
  const transform=/\b(?:rima|poema|versos?|narracion|relato|sintesis|síntesis)\b/.test(p);
  return broad&&transform;
}
function v5MemoryEvidenceRowsFromSnapshot(snapshot={},needle=''){

}

function v5P18HistoricalContractFromMemoryResult(result={},fallbackRows=[]){

  for(const r of arr(fallbackRows)){if(trim(r?._turn_id)||trim(r?.Pregunta))return{source:'memory_evidence',turn_id:trim(r?._turn_id),question:trim(r?.Pregunta),plan_signature:(r?._plan_signature&&typeof r._plan_signature==='object')?r._plan_signature:{},domain:trim(r?._domain),scope:(r?._scope&&typeof r._scope==='object')?r._scope:{}};}
  return null;
}
function v5P18HistoricalContractFromEvidenceRows(rows=[]){

  return null;
}
async function v5P18GeminiMemoryNowPlan({userPrompt,historicalContract,state={},selectedEventId='',conversationHistory=[],clientNowIso='',clientTimeZone='',model,flowTrace=[],externalSignal=null,validationIssues=[],previousPlan=null}={}){
  const hc=historicalContract&&typeof historicalContract==='object'?historicalContract:{},ctx=v5PlannerContextPage(conversationHistory,state,selectedEventId),dialogueState=v5P23ConversationState(conversationHistory,state,selectedEventId),events=arr(state?.eventos).map(e=>trim(e?.titulo||e?.nombre||e?.descripcion)).filter(Boolean).slice(0,80);
  const sys=`Eres Gemini, ÚNICO intérprete semántico de Zuzu. El usuario quiere volver a ejecutar HOY una consulta histórica. Tu trabajo es emitir el contrato ACTUAL semánticamente equivalente a la consulta histórica, usando la API actual de ControlEvent. Devuelve SOLO JSON del esquema.\n\nREGLA SOBERANA: el software NO corregirá tu decisión después. Solo validará legalidad y ejecutará exactamente lo que decidas.\n\nREGLAS MEMORY→NOW:\n- La autoridad para saber QUÉ consulta se repite es HISTORICAL_CONTRACT: especialmente historical_question y plan_signature.\n- El texto de la RESPUESTA histórica NO es autoridad para elegir sujeto, operación ni filtros. Una persona mencionada incidentalmente en la respuesta no se convierte en person/responsible.\n- Conserva el mismo objeto factual, alcance, sujeto y filtros de la consulta histórica, salvo que CURRENT_USER pida explícitamente un cambio.\n- No uses compare_events salvo que la consulta histórica realmente fuera una comparación de eventos. Pedir \"comparar con ahora\" significa mostrar ENTONCES y HOY, no comparar dos eventos.\n- No conviertas compras de un evento en person_profile porque aparezca un responsable dentro de la respuesta antigua.\n- Si el contrato histórico usa un evento concreto, conserva ese evento. Si históricamente era current_event y el usuario solo pide repetirla hoy, usa current_event.\n- Emite un contrato LEGAL según CAPABILITY CONTRACTS. Si scope=all_events, no pongas event.\n- No inventes datos ni entidades.\n\n${cleanCapabilityContractCatalogText()}\n\nAHORA=${trim(clientNowIso)||new Date().toISOString()} zona=${trim(clientTimeZone)||'Europe/Madrid'}\nSCREEN_EVENT=${trim(selectedEventId)||''}\nCONTEXTO_BUENO=${JSON.stringify(ctx)}\nESTADO_DIALOGO=${JSON.stringify(dialogueState)}\nESTADO_DIALOGO SOLO APORTA HECHOS: pending_action, memory_frame, last_successful_action y active_context NO deciden nada por sí mismos. Decide tú qué referencia es pertinente para MENSAJE_ACTUAL. La pregunta inmediatamente anterior de Zuzu y el sentido humano del diálogo tienen prioridad semántica cuando corresponda. El runtime no seleccionará por ti una acción, un recuerdo ni un sujeto.\nACTOR_CANONICO=${JSON.stringify(actorFact)}\nEVENTOS_CANONICOS=${JSON.stringify(events)}`;
  const input=`CURRENT_USER=${userPrompt}\nHISTORICAL_CONTRACT=${JSON.stringify(hc)}${arr(validationIssues).length?`\nCONTRATO_ANTERIOR_INVALIDO=${JSON.stringify(previousPlan||{})}\nERRORES_VALIDACION=${JSON.stringify(validationIssues)}`:''}`;
  const url=`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(model)}:generateContent`,body={systemInstruction:{parts:[{text:sys}]},contents:[{role:'user',parts:[{text:input}]}],generationConfig:{responseMimeType:'application/json',responseSchema:v4BrainLiteSchema(),temperature:.03,maxOutputTokens:700,thinkingConfig:{thinkingBudget:0}}};
  const {res,payload}=await geminiFetchJsonWithTimeout(url,body,geminiKey(),Number(process.env.CONTROLEVENT_ZUZU_BRAINLITE_TIMEOUT_MS||12000),externalSignal);if(!res.ok)throw new Error(payload?.error?.message||`Memory→Now HTTP ${res.status}`);
  const raw=trim(geminiOutText(payload));let plan={};try{plan=JSON.parse(raw)}catch(_){plan={__parse_error:true,intent:'DATA',turn_type:'meta',user_goal:'',response_level:'BASIC',response_goal:{mode:'direct',instruction:''},requires_synthesis:false,state_transition:{kind:'keep',target:''},memory_action:'none',memory_query:'',reply:'',actions:[]};};plan.intent=['DATA','CHAT'].includes(trim(plan.intent).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.intent).toUpperCase():'DATA';plan.response_level=['BASIC','MEDIUM','HIGH'].includes(trim(plan.response_level).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.response_level).toUpperCase():'BASIC';plan.actions=arr(plan.actions).filter(x=>trim(x?.operation));zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'V5 P18 · MEMORY→NOW · GEMINI','OK',`${plan.intent} · ${plan.actions.map(a=>trim(a.operation)).join(', ')||'sin acciones'}`,{historical_contract:hc,decision_contract:v5P15CloneDecisionPlan(plan),model,usage:usageSmall(payload,model)});return plan;
}
function v5P18ValidateMemoryNowPlan(plan={}){const base=v5P16ValidatePlanBeforeFreeze(plan),issues=[...arr(base.issues)];if(trim(plan?.intent).toUpperCase()!=='DATA')issues.push('MEMORY→NOW requiere intent=DATA.');if(!arr(plan?.actions).some(a=>trim(a?.operation)))issues.push('MEMORY→NOW requiere al menos una action factual.');return{ok:issues.length===0,issues};}
function v5P13EvidenceMentionsSubject(row={},subject=''){
  const n=norm(subject);if(!n)return true;const hay=norm([row?.Pregunta,row?.Respuesta,row?.Resumen].filter(Boolean).join(' '));if(!hay)return false;const esc=n.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');return new RegExp(`(?:^|\\s|[^a-z0-9])${esc}(?:$|\\s|[^a-z0-9])`,'i').test(hay);
}
/* FIX41 P18: eliminada la reconstrucción local MEMORY→NOW basada en persona/eventos mencionados. Gemini decide el contrato actual. */
function v5P13NextViewCue(prompt=''){const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);return /^\s*(?:otra|otro|siguiente)(?:\s+grafica|\s+gráfico|\s+vista)?\s*[.!]?\s*$/.test(p);}
function v5P22CancelCue(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);
  return /^\s*(?:no\s*,?\s*)?(?:espera|espere|para|pare|un\s+momento|mejor\s+no|dejalo|déjalo|no\s+lo\s+hagas)\s*[.!]?\s*$/.test(p)||/^\s*no\s*,?\s*espera\s*[.!]?\s*$/.test(p);
}
function v5P22ResumePendingCue(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);
  return v5P13AffirmativeCue(prompt)||/^\s*(?:continua|continúa|sigue|retoma|haz\s+lo\s+de\s+antes|haz\s+eso|lo\s+de\s+antes)\s*[.!]?\s*$/.test(p);
}
function v5P22LastSuccessfulAction(history=[]){
  for(let i=arr(history).length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const t=arr(history)[i]||{},rc=t.resultContext||{},persisted=rc?.conversation_state?.last_successful_action;
    if(persisted&&typeof persisted==='object'&&trim(persisted.operation))return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(persisted));
    const pa=t?.pendingAction&&typeof t.pendingAction==='object'?t.pendingAction:null;
    if(pa&&trim(pa.status)==='completed'&&trim(pa.type)==='REEXECUTE_MEMORY_NOW'&&pa.current_contract&&typeof pa.current_contract==='object'){
      const first=arr(pa.current_contract.actions)[0]||{};
      return{type:'REEXECUTE_MEMORY_NOW',operation:trim(first.operation),event:trim(first.event),person:trim(first.person),contract:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(pa.current_contract)),current_contract:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(pa.current_contract)),historical_contract:(pa.historical_contract&&typeof pa.historical_contract==='object')?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(pa.historical_contract)):null,subject:trim(pa.subject),source_turn_id:trim(pa.source_turn_id),source_conversation_id:trim(pa.source_conversation_id)};
    }
    const op=trim(rc.operation),acts=arr(rc.last_query_actions);
    if(op&&!['recall_memory','current_time','current_date'].includes(op))return{type:'FACTUAL',operation:op,event:trim(rc.event),person:trim(rc.person),contract:(rc.memory_current_contract&&typeof rc.memory_current_contract==='object')?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rc.memory_current_contract)):null};
    if(acts.length)return{type:'FACTUAL',operation:trim(acts[0]?.operation),event:trim(acts[0]?.event),person:trim(acts[0]?.person),contract:null};
  }
  return null;
}
function v5P24RepeatLastCue(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt);
  return /^\s*(?:haz\s+lo\s+de\s+antes|lo\s+de\s+antes|otra\s+vez|repitelo|repítelo|repite\s+lo\s+anterior|como\s+antes|vuelve\s+a\s+hacer\s+eso)\s*[.!]?\s*$/.test(p);
}
function v5P23PendingFromMemoryTurn(mem=null,status='pending'){
  const mp=mem?.memoryPresentation||{};
  if(!mem||!mp?.canRefresh||!(mp.historicalContract&&typeof mp.historicalContract==='object'))return null;
  return{type:'REEXECUTE_MEMORY_NOW',status:trim(status)||'pending',subject:trim(mem.person||mp.subject),historical_contract:JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(mp.historicalContract)),source_turn_id:trim(mp.historicalContract?.turn_id||mem?.resultContext?.matched_turn_id),source_conversation_id:trim(mem?.resultContext?.conversation_id),memory_age_text:trim(mp.ageText),memory_occurred_at:trim(mp.occurredAt)};
}
function v5P23PendingStateFromHistory(history=[]){
  for(let i=arr(history).length-1;i>=0;i--){
    const t=arr(history)[i]||{},candidate=(t.pendingAction&&typeof t.pendingAction==='object')?t.pendingAction:((t?.resultContext?.conversation_state?.pending_action&&typeof t.resultContext.conversation_state.pending_action==='object')?t.resultContext.conversation_state.pending_action:null);
    if(!candidate||!trim(candidate.type))continue;
    const c=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(candidate)),st=trim(c.status)||'pending';
    if(['completed','cancelled'].includes(st))return{action:null,terminal:true,last:c};
    if(['pending','suspended'].includes(st))return{action:c,terminal:false,last:c};
  }
  return{action:null,terminal:false,last:null};
}
function v5P23ConversationState(history=[],state={},selectedEventId=''){
  const mem=v5P11LastMemoryTurn(history),last=v5P22LastSuccessfulAction(history),mp=mem?.memoryPresentation||{},ps=v5P23PendingStateFromHistory(history);
  const pending=ps.action||(!ps.terminal?v5P23PendingFromMemoryTurn(mem,'pending'):null);
  return{active_context:v4BrainLiteContext(history,state,selectedEventId),memory_frame:mem?{open:true,subject:trim(mem.person||mp.subject),age_text:trim(mp.ageText),historical_contract:(mp.historicalContract&&typeof mp.historicalContract==='object')?mp.historicalContract:null,source_turn_id:trim(mp.historicalContract?.turn_id||mem?.resultContext?.matched_turn_id),source_conversation_id:trim(mem?.resultContext?.conversation_id)}:null,pending_action:pending,last_successful_action:last};
}
function v5P22ConversationState(history=[],state={},selectedEventId=''){return v5P23ConversationState(history,state,selectedEventId);}
function v5ChartRequestOnly(prompt=''){
  const p=vnextP17LooseNorm(prompt),sem=v4BrainLitePromptSemantics(prompt),sig=v5PromptRouteSignals(prompt),hasView=sem.chartFollow||/\b(?:graf\w*|barras?|lineas?|donut|queso|tarta)\b/.test(p);if(!hasView)return false;
  // P6: una reactivación/anáfora + cambio de presentación sigue siendo VIEW_ONLY.
  // Un objeto factual NUEVO (p.ej. compras por responsable + donut) no lo es.
  const anaphoric=/\b(?:mism\w*|es[ea]s?|aquell\w*|esos\s+datos|ese\s+desglose|ese\s+resumen|ponla|hazlo|representa(?:lo|la)?|cambia(?:lo|la)?)\b/.test(p);
  const reportViewRef=sem.report&&sem.chartFollow&&/\b(?:el|este|ese|aquel)\s+(?:dossier|informe|resumen)\b/.test(p);
  const factualModifier=sem.responsibleBreakdown||sem.attendeeIncome||sem.attendanceAll||sem.cashOnly||(sem.report&&!reportViewRef)||sem.topResponsible||((sem.pending||sem.realized)&&sem.purchase);
  if(factualModifier)return false;if(!sig.domains.length)return true;if(sig.presentation&&(sig.reopen||!!sig.anchor_domain||anaphoric))return true;
  return sig.presentation&&sig.domains.length===1&&/^(?:\s*(?:ahora\s+)?(?:de\s+)?(?:barras?|lineas?|donut|queso|tarta|graf\w*)\s*)$/i.test(p);
}
function v5ChartsFromSnapshot(snapshot={},chartType='bar'){
  const charts=[];for(const o of arr(snapshot?.operations)){if(!o?.evidence)continue;const item={args:{...(o.args||{}),operation:trim(o.operation||o.args?.operation),chart:true,chart_type:chartType},result:o.evidence};charts.push(...v5GenericChartsFromItem(item));if(trim(o.operation)==='event_weather')charts.push(...v73WeatherChartFromResult(o.evidence).map(ch=>({...ch,type:chartType})));if(trim(o.operation)==='compare_events')charts.push(...v4BrainLiteCompareCharts(o.evidence).map(ch=>({...ch,type:chartType})));}
  const uniq=[];for(const ch of charts){const k=`${trim(ch?.title)}|${chartType}|${JSON.stringify(ch?.labels||[])}`;if(!uniq.some(x=>x.k===k))uniq.push({k,ch:{...ch,type:chartType}});}return uniq.slice(0,6).map(x=>x.ch);
}
function v5ViewOnlyFrame(prev={},chartType='bar',charts=[]){
  const contexts=arr(prev?.contexts).map(c=>v5EnsureContextShape({...c,contexts:undefined},trim(prev?.screen_event),num(c?.last_used_seq))),idx=contexts.findIndex(c=>trim(c.context_id)===trim(prev?.active_context_id));if(idx<0)return prev;
  const c=contexts[idx],ps=v5ActiveSnapshot(c);if(!ps)return prev;const seq=Math.max(num(prev?.last_used_seq),...contexts.map(x=>num(x.last_used_seq)))+1,ordinal=arr(c.snapshots).length+1;
  const ops=arr(ps.operations).map(o=>({...o,args:{...(o.args||{})},evidence:o.evidence?{...o.evidence,tables:arr(o.evidence.tables).map(t=>({...t,rows:arr(t.rows).map(r=>({...r}))}))}:undefined}));
  const snap={...ps,snapshot_id:`s_${v5ContextHash(c.context_id).slice(0,6)}_${seq}_${ordinal}`,parent_snapshot_id:trim(ps.snapshot_id),created_seq:seq,operations:ops,response_blocks:v5ResponseBlocksFromOperations(ops),presentation:{chart:true,chart_type:chartType},views:[{view_id:'v_text',type:'text',source_operation_ids:ops.map(o=>o.operation_id)},...arr(charts).map((ch,i)=>({view_id:`v_chart_${i+1}`,type:trim(ch?.type)||chartType,source_operation_ids:ops.map(o=>o.operation_id),title:trim(ch.title)}))],answer_shape:'visual'};
  const snapshots=[...arr(c.snapshots),snap].slice(-6);contexts[idx]={...c,snapshots,active_snapshot_id:snap.snapshot_id,last_used_seq:seq,chart:true,chart_type:chartType};return{...v5ProjectSnapshotToLegacy(contexts[idx]),contexts,active_context_id:c.context_id,active_snapshot_id:snap.snapshot_id,screen_event:trim(prev.screen_event)};
}
// V5.3 SOVEREIGN SEMANTIC COMPILER: CE aporta hechos/estado; Gemini interpreta TODO el lenguaje.
async function v4BrainLitePlan({userPrompt,state,selectedEventId,conversationHistory=[],clientNowIso,clientTimeZone,conversationLanguage='es-ES',model,flowTrace=[],externalSignal=null}={}){
  const actor=state?.usuarioLogado||state?.ce_acceso_usuario_logado||{};
  const actorProfile=zuzuLoggedUserProfile({usuarioLogado:actor});
  const actorFact={id:trim(actor?.id||actor?.identificacion||actor?.Identificacion),name:trim(actorProfile?.nombre||actor?.name),login:trim(actorProfile?.identificacion||actor?.login||actor?.username||actor?.email),role:trim(actorProfile?.nivel||actor?.rol||actor?.role),authoritative:true};
  const screenEvent=trim(v26EventById(state,selectedEventId)?.titulo),threads=v53ThreadGraph(conversationHistory,state,selectedEventId),ledger=v53EvidenceLedger(conversationHistory),recent=v53RecentDialogue(conversationHistory),ontology=v53LogicalOntology(),entityIndex=v532CompactEntityIndex(state);
  const sys=`Eres el COMPILADOR SEMÁNTICO soberano de Zuzu dentro de ControlEvent. Eres el único componente que interpreta el lenguaje humano. ControlEvent no aplicará regex, listas de palabras, sinónimos ni if/else lingüísticos para decidir intención, referencia, sujeto, hilo o herramienta.

OBJETIVO: traducir cualquier mensaje humano al contrato JSON del esquema, usando solo hechos estructurados y la ontología lógica. No inventes datos, entidades, relaciones ni capacidades.

ARQUITECTURA:
- SESSION son hechos ambientales, nunca una orden implícita.
- THREAD_GRAPH contiene asuntos activos/suspendidos. Tú decides si mantener, actualizar, crear, cambiar, suspender o reanudar un hilo.
- EVIDENCE_LEDGER contiene METADATOS de resultados certificados anteriores. Si necesitas el contenido de una evidencia ya existente para razonar, pon su ID en required_materials y NO inventes su contenido.
- RECENT_DIALOGUE conserva únicamente los últimos turnos crudos para resolver elipsis, tono y referencias humanas.
- DATA_ONTOLOGY describe el universo lógico y los adaptadores canónicos disponibles. Las operaciones backend son transitorias y solo sirven para obtener hechos; NO son reglas de lenguaje.
- ENTITY_INDEX es un índice compacto y autoritativo de nombres existentes (solo ID+nombre). Úsalo para distinguir tipos y elegir entidades reales; no contiene datos de negocio. CE seguirá resolviendo mecánicamente el nombre/ID elegido.
- SESSION.actor es la identidad autenticada actual y es un HECHO autoritativo cuando tenga id/name/login. Si el usuario pregunta por sí mismo, puedes responder con esos datos sin consultar CE.
- OUTPUT_LANGUAGE es vinculante para reply y para cualquier redacción posterior, salvo que el usuario pida explícitamente cambiar el idioma dentro del propio mensaje.

REGLAS GENERALES, NO BASADAS EN FRASES:
1. Interpreta el objetivo humano completo y su referencia conversacional usando RECENT_DIALOGUE + THREAD_GRAPH.
2. Si la respuesta requiere hechos no disponibles, solicita actions suficientes para obtenerlos. Un adapter con coverage=overview NO prueba por sí solo dominios detallados que la conclusión necesite; para una conclusión multidominio pide las evidencias de dominio necesarias. Si ya existe evidencia certificada pertinente, reutilízala mediante evidence_used / required_materials en vez de consultar de nuevo.
3. Para análisis complejos, exige toda la evidencia necesaria antes de concluir. La extracción/cálculo es de CE; la inferencia y explicación son tuyas.
4. No arrastres un sujeto previo por defecto. Solo conserva entidades anteriores cuando semánticamente formen parte del mismo hilo según tu interpretación.
5. No expongas nombres físicos de tablas, SQL, datasets internos ni detalles de implementación.
6. Las métricas canónicas las calcula CE; no reconstruyas fórmulas internas si la ontología dice que son canónicas.
7. Si SESSION, THREAD_GRAPH, RECENT_DIALOGUE o RESOLVED_MATERIALS ya contienen todos los hechos necesarios, turn_result=ANSWER, intent=CHAT, actions=[] y reply responde realmente al usuario. No ordenes otra extracción ni uses un narrador posterior. No devuelvas promesas vacías.
8. Si necesitas materiales previos, turn_result=NEED_MATERIALS y required_materials debe contener solo IDs presentes en EVIDENCE_LEDGER. Cuando RESOLVED_MATERIALS llegue y sea suficiente, responde directamente con ANSWER; NO repitas la extracción que originó esa evidencia.
9. Si el usuario pide modificar/presentar/continuar el resultado activo y THREAD_GRAPH identifica dataset_evidence_id/current_view/last_operation, resuelve la intención como acción o reutilización sobre ese hilo; no conviertas una petición ya comprendida en una pregunta de confirmación.
10. thread_mutation debe ser una decisión concreta; nunca devuelvas una opción ambigua del tipo mantener-o-cambiar.
11. turn_result debe reflejar el cierre real: ANSWER=respuesta suficiente ahora; EXECUTE=hay actions; NEED_MATERIALS=hay required_materials; CLARIFY=solo si falta información que no puede inferirse de SESSION/THREADS/ENTITY_INDEX.
12. Devuelve siempre el contrato completo y SOLO JSON.

DATA_ONTOLOGY=${JSON.stringify(ontology)}
OUTPUT_LANGUAGE=${trim(conversationLanguage)||'es-ES'}`;
  const basePayload={session:{actor:actorFact,language:trim(conversationLanguage)||'es-ES',now:trim(clientNowIso)||new Date().toISOString(),timezone:trim(clientTimeZone)||'Europe/Madrid',current_screen_event:screenEvent||null,ontology_version:ontology.ontology_version},thread_graph:threads,evidence_ledger:ledger,entity_index:entityIndex,recent_dialogue:recent,user_prompt:userPrompt};
  const call=async(resolvedMaterials={})=>{
    const input=JSON.stringify({...basePayload,resolved_materials:resolvedMaterials});
    const url=`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(model)}:generateContent`,body={systemInstruction:{parts:[{text:sys}]},contents:[{role:'user',parts:[{text:input}]}],generationConfig:{responseMimeType:'application/json',responseSchema:v4BrainLiteSchema(),temperature:.04,maxOutputTokens:620,thinkingConfig:{thinkingBudget:0}}};
    const {res,payload}=await geminiFetchJsonWithTimeout(url,body,geminiKey(),Number(process.env.CONTROLEVENT_ZUZU_BRAINLITE_TIMEOUT_MS||12000),externalSignal);if(!res.ok)throw new Error(payload?.error?.message||`Semantic compiler HTTP ${res.status}`);
    const raw=trim(geminiOutText(payload));let plan={};try{plan=JSON.parse(raw)}catch(_){plan={__parse_error:true,turn_result:'CLARIFY',intent:'CHAT',turn_type:'meta',user_goal:'',response_level:'BASIC',response_goal:{mode:'direct',instruction:''},requires_synthesis:false,thread_mutation:{action:'KEEP_THREAD',target_thread_id:threads.active_thread_id,updated_goal:''},state_transition:{kind:'keep',target:''},memory_action:'none',memory_query:'',language:trim(conversationLanguage)||'es-ES',required_materials:[],evidence_used:[],reply:'',actions:[]};}
    plan.turn_result=['ANSWER','EXECUTE','NEED_MATERIALS','CLARIFY'].includes(trim(plan.turn_result).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.turn_result).toUpperCase():(arr(plan.required_materials).length?'NEED_MATERIALS':(arr(plan.actions).length?'EXECUTE':'ANSWER'));plan.intent=['DATA','CHAT'].includes(trim(plan.intent).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.intent).toUpperCase():'CHAT';plan.response_level=['BASIC','MEDIUM','HIGH'].includes(trim(plan.response_level).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.response_level).toUpperCase():'BASIC';plan.actions=arr(plan.actions).filter(x=>trim(x?.operation));plan.turn_type=trim(plan.turn_type)||((plan.intent==='CHAT')?'social':'factual');plan.user_goal=trim(plan.user_goal);plan.response_goal=(plan.response_goal&&typeof plan.response_goal==='object')?plan.response_goal:{mode:'direct',instruction:''};plan.requires_synthesis=plan.requires_synthesis===true;plan.thread_mutation=(plan.thread_mutation&&typeof plan.thread_mutation==='object')?plan.thread_mutation:{action:'KEEP_THREAD',target_thread_id:threads.active_thread_id,updated_goal:''};plan.state_transition=(plan.state_transition&&typeof plan.state_transition==='object')?plan.state_transition:{kind:'keep',target:''};plan.memory_action=trim(plan.memory_action)||'none';plan.memory_query=trim(plan.memory_query);plan.language=trim(plan.language)||trim(conversationLanguage)||'es-ES';plan.required_materials=arr(plan.required_materials).map(trim).filter(Boolean);plan.evidence_used=arr(plan.evidence_used).map(trim).filter(Boolean);return{plan,payload};
  };
  let first=await call({}),plan=first.plan,payload=first.payload;
  if(plan.required_materials.length){
    const materials=v53MaterializeEvidence(conversationHistory,plan.required_materials),missing=plan.required_materials.filter(id=>!materials[id]);
    if(!missing.length){const second=await call(materials);plan=second.plan;payload=second.payload;zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'V5.3 · LAZY EVIDENCE','OK',`Materializadas ${Object.keys(materials).length} evidencia(s) solicitadas por Gemini.`);}else zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'V5.3 · LAZY EVIDENCE','WARN',`Gemini pidió evidencias no disponibles: ${missing.join(', ')}`);
  }
  zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'V5.3 · SEMANTIC COMPILER','OK',`${plan.intent} · hilo=${trim(plan?.thread_mutation?.action)}:${trim(plan?.thread_mutation?.target_thread_id)} · acciones=${plan.actions.map(x=>trim(x.operation)).join(', ')||'ninguna'} · evidencias=${plan.evidence_used.join(', ')||'—'}`,{model,ontology_version:ontology.ontology_version,thread_graph:{active_thread_id:threads.active_thread_id,count:threads.threads.length},evidence_count:ledger.length,usage:usageSmall(payload,model)});return{plan,payload};
}

// ============================================================================
// V5 CLEAN AUTHORITY P15 · GEMINI DECISION CONTRACT INMUTABLE
// Gemini es el único intérprete semántico de la ruta factual ordinaria.
// Después de su JSON, ninguna guardia legacy puede cambiar dominio, operación, scope,
// sujeto, evento, filtros ni vista. Solo se permiten normalización de tipo/sintaxis,
// resolución canónica de una entidad ya decidida y derivación técnica de current_event.
// ============================================================================
function v5P15CloneDecisionPlan(plan={}){
  const rg=plan?.response_goal&&typeof plan.response_goal==='object'?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(plan.response_goal)):{mode:'direct',instruction:''};
  const st=plan?.state_transition&&typeof plan.state_transition==='object'?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(plan.state_transition)):{kind:'keep',target:''};
  return{turn_result:['ANSWER','EXECUTE','NEED_MATERIALS','CLARIFY'].includes(trim(plan?.turn_result).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.turn_result).toUpperCase():(arr(plan?.required_materials).length?'NEED_MATERIALS':(arr(plan?.actions).length?'EXECUTE':'ANSWER')),intent:['DATA','CHAT'].includes(trim(plan?.intent).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.intent).toUpperCase():'CHAT',turn_type:trim(plan?.turn_type)||'factual',user_goal:trim(plan?.user_goal),response_level:['BASIC','MEDIUM','HIGH'].includes(trim(plan?.response_level).toUpperCase())?trim(plan.response_level).toUpperCase():'BASIC',response_goal:rg,requires_synthesis:plan?.requires_synthesis===true,thread_mutation:(plan?.thread_mutation&&typeof plan.thread_mutation==='object')?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(plan.thread_mutation)):{action:'KEEP_THREAD',target_thread_id:'',updated_goal:''},state_transition:st,memory_action:trim(plan?.memory_action)||'none',memory_query:trim(plan?.memory_query),language:trim(plan?.language)||'es-ES',required_materials:arr(plan?.required_materials).map(trim).filter(Boolean),evidence_used:arr(plan?.evidence_used).map(trim).filter(Boolean),reply:trim(plan?.reply),actions:arr(plan?.actions).filter(x=>trim(x?.operation)).map(a=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(a)))};
}
function v5P15DecisionFingerprint(plan={}){return JSON.stringify(v5P15CloneDecisionPlan(plan));}
function v5P15ValidateFrozenAction(action={}){
  const checked=validateCleanDecisionContract(action);
  if(!checked.ok){const e=new Error(checked.issues.join(' | '));e.code='CLEAN_CONTRACT_INVALID';e.contractIssues=checked.issues;throw e;}
  return true;
}
function v5P16ValidatePlanBeforeFreeze(plan={}){
  const issues=[];
  if(plan?.__parse_error===true)issues.push('La salida estructurada de Gemini no era JSON válido; Gemini debe reemitirla sin reparación local.');
  if(!trim(plan?.user_goal))issues.push('Falta user_goal soberano.');
  if(!trim(plan?.response_goal?.mode))issues.push('Falta response_goal.mode soberano.');
  const tr=trim(plan?.turn_result).toUpperCase();if(!['ANSWER','EXECUTE','NEED_MATERIALS','CLARIFY'].includes(tr))issues.push('Falta turn_result soberano válido.');
  if(tr==='ANSWER'&&arr(plan?.actions).length)issues.push('turn_result=ANSWER no puede incluir actions.');
  if(tr==='EXECUTE'&&!arr(plan?.actions).length)issues.push('turn_result=EXECUTE requiere actions.');
  if(tr==='NEED_MATERIALS'&&!arr(plan?.required_materials).length)issues.push('turn_result=NEED_MATERIALS requiere required_materials.');
  if(issues.length)return{ok:false,issues};
  if(trim(plan?.intent).toUpperCase()!=='DATA')return{ok:true,issues:[]};
  const actions=arr(plan?.actions).filter(x=>trim(x?.operation));
  if(!actions.length)issues.push('intent=DATA requiere al menos una action.');
  actions.forEach((a,i)=>{const v=validateCleanDecisionContract(a);for(const issue of v.issues)issues.push(`action[${i}]: ${issue}`);});
  return{ok:issues.length===0,issues};
}
async function v5P16GeminiRepairInvalidPlan({plan,userPrompt,state,selectedEventId,conversationHistory=[],clientNowIso,clientTimeZone,conversationLanguage='es-ES',model,flowTrace=[],externalSignal=null,issues=[]}={}){
  const ontology=v53LogicalOntology(),threads=v53ThreadGraph(conversationHistory,state,selectedEventId),ledger=v53EvidenceLedger(conversationHistory),entityIndex=v532CompactEntityIndex(state),actor=state?.usuarioLogado||state?.ce_acceso_usuario_logado||{};
