    toolMs=Date.now()-ts;const good=results.filter(x=>x.result),bad=results.filter(x=>x.error);
    if(good.length){
      const parts=[];for(const x of good){const name=trim(x.call?.name);if(name==='query_ce'){const st=await ensureState();parts.push(vnextP19LocalFinal(x.result,x.args,st));}else if(name==='resolve_entity')parts.push(vnextP11ResolveFinal(x.result));else if(name==='search_documents')parts.push(vnextP11DocsFinal(x.result));else if(name==='dialogue_state')parts.push(vnextP123DialogueFinal(x.result));else parts.push(vnextP11MemoryFinal(x.result,userPrompt,x.args));}
      final={title:parts.length===1?parts[0].title:'Zuzu',answer:[...new Set(parts.map(x=>trim(x.answer)).filter(Boolean))].join('\n\n'),warnings:bad.map(x=>x.error)};
      const currentSummary=good.length===1&&trim(good[0]?.call?.name)==='recall_memory'&&trim(good[0]?.args?.action||good[0]?.result?.facts?.action)==='current'?good[0]:null,memoryEpisodeSummary=good.length===1&&trim(good[0]?.call?.name)==='recall_memory'&&trim(good[0]?.args?.action||good[0]?.result?.facts?.action)==='summarize'?good[0]:null,currentDatasetSummary=good.length===1&&trim(good[0]?.call?.name)==='query_ce'&&trim(good[0]?.result?._vnext_operation||good[0]?.args?.operation)==='summarize_current'?good[0]:null,narratable=good.find(x=>x?.args?.narrate===true);
      if(currentSummary){const n0=Date.now();try{const n=await vnextP122SummarizeCurrentConversation({userPrompt,model,result:currentSummary.result,flowTrace,externalSignal,voiceConversation});calls++;narrationMs=Date.now()-n0;final={...final,title:'Resumen de la conversación',answer:trim(n?.answer)||vnextP1221LocalConversationSummary(currentSummary.result)};payloadId=trim(n?.payload?.id)||payloadId;}catch(error){narrationMs=Date.now()-n0;final={...final,title:'Resumen de la conversación',answer:vnextP1221LocalConversationSummary(currentSummary.result)};zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.22.2 · resumen conversación actual','WARN',`Falló la narración IA; se entrega resumen local factual: ${cleanGeminiError(error)}`);}}
      else if(memoryEpisodeSummary){const n0=Date.now();try{const n=await vnextP1223SummarizeMemoryEpisode({userPrompt,model,result:memoryEpisodeSummary.result,flowTrace,externalSignal,voiceConversation});calls++;narrationMs=Date.now()-n0;final={...final,title:'Resumen de la conversación recordada',answer:trim(n?.answer)||vnextP1221LocalConversationSummary(memoryEpisodeSummary.result)};payloadId=trim(n?.payload?.id)||payloadId;}catch(error){narrationMs=Date.now()-n0;final={...final,title:'Resumen de la conversación recordada',answer:vnextP1221LocalConversationSummary(memoryEpisodeSummary.result)};zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.22.3 · resumen episodio histórico','WARN',`Falló la narración IA; se entrega resumen local factual: ${cleanGeminiError(error)}`);}}
      else if(currentDatasetSummary){const n0=Date.now();try{const n=await vnextP1222SummarizeCurrentDataset({userPrompt,model,result:currentDatasetSummary.result,flowTrace,externalSignal,voiceConversation});calls++;narrationMs=Date.now()-n0;final={...final,title:'Resumen del dataset actual',answer:trim(n?.answer)||vnextP1222LocalDatasetSummary(currentDatasetSummary.result)};payloadId=trim(n?.payload?.id)||payloadId;}catch(error){narrationMs=Date.now()-n0;final={...final,title:'Resumen del dataset actual',answer:vnextP1222LocalDatasetSummary(currentDatasetSummary.result)};zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.22.3 · resumen dataset actual','WARN',`Falló la narración IA; se entrega resumen local factual: ${cleanGeminiError(error)}`);}}
      else if(narratable&&payloadId){const n0=Date.now();try{const n=await vnextP13NarrateToolResult({userPrompt,payloadId,model,systemInstruction,call:narratable.call,result:narratable.result,flowTrace,externalSignal,voiceConversation});calls++;narrationMs=Date.now()-n0;if(trim(n?.answer))final={...final,title:trim(narratable?.result?.title)||final.title,answer:trim(n.answer)};payloadId=trim(n?.payload?.id)||payloadId;}catch(error){zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.9 · narración excepcional','WARN',`Se conserva cierre local: ${cleanGeminiError(error)}`);}}
    }else{const msg=[...new Set(bad.map(x=>x.error).filter(Boolean))].join(' · ');final={title:'No tengo ese dato cerrado',answer:`No he podido obtener ese dato con una fuente que lo acredite exactamente${msg?`: ${msg}`:'.'}`,warnings:[...new Set(bad.map(x=>x.error).filter(Boolean))]};}
  }
  let answer=v29SanitizeAnswerMarkup(vnextP1222StripInternalMetadata(trim(final.answer)));if(!answer&&trim(final.answer))answer='He mantenido el contexto interno de trabajo sin exponerlo en la respuesta.';
  const humanState=state||{},lastGood=[...results].reverse().find(x=>x?.result),lastData=[...results].reverse().find(x=>x?.result&&trim(x?.call?.name)==='query_ce'),socialDecision=lastData?.args||lastGood?.args||{},socialRegister=vnextP15SocialRegister(socialDecision),socialSpeech=lastData?vnextP19SpokenAnswer(lastData.result,lastData.args,humanState,userPrompt):vnextP15AuxSpokenAnswer(lastGood,answer,humanState,userPrompt),unitGuard=v416VoiceUnitOracle(socialSpeech||answer,answer,humanState,null),spokenHuman=humanizeSpokenEntities(unitGuard.spoken||socialSpeech||answer,humanState,{currentDate:clientLocalDateTime||clientNowIso,seed:`vnextp15|${payloadId||currentPrev}|${userPrompt}`}),spokenAnswer=v40ConversationalPolish(spokenHuman.text,userPrompt,true),usage=summarizeGeminiUsageFromTrace(flowTrace),totalMs=Date.now()-started;
  zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.10 · REGISTRO ORAL','OK',`nivel=${socialRegister} · tease=${socialDecision?.tease===true?'sí':'no'} · pantalla=factual · voz=${socialSpeech?'social':'directa'}.`);
  const nextInteractionId=hadTools?'':(payloadId||currentPrev),resetInteractionId=hadTools,resultContext=vnextP1222ContextFromResults(results,final,conversationHistory,userPrompt);
  zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.10 · LATENCIA','OK',`total=${totalMs} ms · IA=${modelMs} ms · espera estado tras IA=${stateWaitMs} ms · datos=${toolMs} ms · narración excepcional=${narrationMs} ms · llamadas IA=${calls} · contratos=${functionCalls.length} · tokens=${num(usage?.totalTokens)} · coste≈${num(usage?.costEurApprox).toFixed(6)} €.`);
  const outputTables=tables.filter(Boolean).slice(0,8),persistentTables=outputTables.length?outputTables:vnextP1222PersistentTables(resultContext,flowTrace),finalTables=persistentTables.filter(Boolean).slice(0,8),presentationEvidence=vnextP124PresentationEvidence(finalTables,charts.slice(0,8));
  zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.27 · PRESENTACIÓN MATERIALIZADA','OK',`tablas=${presentationEvidence.tableCount} · gráficas=${presentationEvidence.chartCount} · filas=${presentationEvidence.tables.reduce((n,t)=>n+num(t.rowCount),0)}.`);
  return{ok:true,rejected:false,title:trim(final.title)||'Zuzu VNext',answer,spokenAnswer,warnings:arr(final.warnings),charts:charts.slice(0,8),tables:finalTables,files:[],provider:'zuzu-vnext-p127-natural-visible-content-dialogue',model,interactionId:nextInteractionId,conversationId:trim(conversationId),meta:{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),version:'v5.2_exp',architecture:'VNext P1.27 · contenido visible narrable + columnas completas + vista canónica + nombres ITV por batería · NHC',experimental:true,voiceConversation:!!voiceConversation,interactionId:nextInteractionId,resetInteractionId,spokenAnswer,resultContext,presentationEvidence,capabilityRegistryVersion:CAPABILITY_REGISTRY_VERSION,capabilityCalls:results.map(x=>({tool:trim(x?.call?.name),rawArgs:x?.rawArgs||{},normalizedArgs:x?.args||{},effectiveOperation:trim(x?.result?._vnext_operation||x?.result?.facts?.operation||x?.capabilityAudit?.effectiveOperation||x?.args?.operation),effectiveSubject:{event:trim(x?.result?.facts?.event||x?.args?.event),person:trim(x?.result?.facts?.person||x?.args?.person)},durationMs:num(x?.durationMs),audit:x?.capabilityAudit||null,envelope:x?.capabilityAudit?.envelope||null,error:trim(x?.error)})),tools:[...new Set(results.map(x=>trim(x?.call?.name)).filter(Boolean))],performance:{totalMs,decisionModelMs:modelMs,stateWaitAfterModelMs:stateWaitMs,dataMs:toolMs,narrationModelMs:narrationMs,interactionCalls:calls,contractCalls:functionCalls.length},geminiUsageEstimate:usage,debugTrace:arr(flowTrace).slice(0,120)},debugTrace:arr(flowTrace).slice(0,120),showDebugTrace:true};
}

async function runZuzuVNextP11Agent({userPrompt,state,selectedEventId,flowTrace=[],previousInteractionId='',conversationHistory=[],voiceConversation=false,usuarioLogado,user,authUser,ce_acceso,clientNowIso,clientLocalDateTime,clientTimeZone,externalSignal=null,conversationId=''}){
  const started=Date.now(),actor=state?.usuarioLogado||state?.ce_acceso_usuario_logado||usuarioLogado||user||authUser||ce_acceso||{},model=(configuredGeminiModelsForTask('zuzu-structured')[0]||'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'),tools=vnextP11Tools(),systemInstruction=vnextP11SystemInstruction(state,selectedEventId,{usuarioLogado,user,authUser,ce_acceso,voiceConversation,clientLocalDateTime});
  let currentPrev=trim(previousInteractionId),payload,calls=0,modelMs=0,toolMs=0,hadTools=false,final={title:'Zuzu',answer:'',warnings:[]},tables=[],results=[];
  zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.1 · NATIVE TOOL FAST PATH','OK',`1 Interaction IA por turno factual + cierre local; predecessor=${currentPrev?'sí':'no'}; sin Semantic Core, sin Memory Gate, sin segunda IA.`);
  const input=currentPrev?userPrompt:vnextP1Input(userPrompt,conversationHistory),m1=Date.now();
  try{payload=await v261CallInteraction({input,previousInteractionId:currentPrev,model,systemInstruction,tools,flowTrace,stage:'VNEXT P1.1 · Gemini conversa/elige contrato',externalSignal,maxCalls:2,maxOutputTokens:voiceConversation?700:1000});calls++;}
  catch(error){if(currentPrev&&v261PreviousIdFailure(error)){zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.1 · predecessor','WARN','La Interaction anterior expiró; se usa el puente local de los últimos turnos.');currentPrev='';payload=await v261CallInteraction({input:vnextP1Input(userPrompt,conversationHistory),model,systemInstruction,tools,flowTrace,stage:'VNEXT P1.1 · recupera hilo',externalSignal,maxCalls:2,maxOutputTokens:voiceConversation?700:1000});calls++;}else throw error;}
  modelMs=Date.now()-m1;const payloadId=trim(payload?.id),functionCalls=v261FunctionCalls(payload);hadTools=functionCalls.length>0;
  if(!hadTools){
    const parsed=v261ParseFinal(payload),raw=trim(parsed?.answer)||trim(v261OutputText(payload));
    final={title:trim(parsed?.title)||'Zuzu',answer:raw||'No he recibido contenido útil para contestarte. Repite solo esta pregunta y la proceso de nuevo.',warnings:arr(parsed?.warnings)};
  }else{
    const ts=Date.now();
    for(const call of functionCalls){
      try{
        let result,name=trim(call?.name),args=(call?.arguments&&typeof call.arguments==='object')?call.arguments:{};
        if(name==='query_ce')result=await vnextP1ExecuteData(args,state,selectedEventId,flowTrace);
        else if(name==='resolve_entity')result=vnextResolveTool(call,state);
        else if(name==='search_documents')result=await vnextSearchDocuments(call,state,selectedEventId,flowTrace);
        else if(name==='recall_memory')result=await vnextRecallMemory(call,actor,conversationId,clientLocalDateTime||clientNowIso);
        else throw new Error(`Contrato VNext desconocido: ${name}.`);
        results.push({call,result,args});tables.push(...vnextP1LocalPresentation(result));
        zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,`VNEXT P1.1 · ${name}`,'OK',`${trim(result?.title)||'Resultado'} · operación=${trim(result?._vnext_operation||args?.operation)||'—'} · filas=${arr(result?.tables).reduce((n,t)=>n+arr(t?.rows).length,0)}.`);
      }catch(error){const msg=cleanGeminiError(error);results.push({call,error:msg,args:(call?.arguments&&typeof call.arguments==='object')?call.arguments:{}});zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,`VNEXT P1.1 · ${trim(call?.name)}`,'WARN',msg);}
    }
    toolMs=Date.now()-ts;const good=results.filter(x=>x.result),bad=results.filter(x=>x.error);
    if(good.length){const parts=good.map(x=>trim(x.call?.name)==='query_ce'?vnextP1LocalFinal(x.result,x.args,state):trim(x.call?.name)==='resolve_entity'?vnextP11ResolveFinal(x.result):trim(x.call?.name)==='search_documents'?vnextP11DocsFinal(x.result):vnextP11MemoryFinal(x.result));final={title:parts.length===1?parts[0].title:'Zuzu',answer:parts.map(x=>trim(x.answer)).filter(Boolean).join('\n\n'),warnings:bad.map(x=>x.error)};}
    else{const msg=bad.map(x=>x.error).filter(Boolean).join(' · ');final={title:'No tengo ese dato cerrado',answer:`No he podido obtener ese dato con una fuente que lo acredite exactamente${msg?`: ${msg}`:'.'}`,warnings:bad.map(x=>x.error)};}
  }
  let answer=v29SanitizeAnswerMarkup(vnextP1222StripInternalMetadata(trim(final.answer)));if(!answer&&trim(final.answer))answer='He mantenido el contexto interno de trabajo sin exponerlo en la respuesta.';if(voiceConversation)answer=v40ConversationalPolish(answer,userPrompt,true);const unitGuard=v416VoiceUnitOracle(answer,answer,state,null),spokenHuman=humanizeSpokenEntities(unitGuard.spoken||answer,state,{currentDate:clientLocalDateTime||clientNowIso,seed:`vnextp11|${payloadId||currentPrev}|${userPrompt}`}),spokenAnswer=v40ConversationalPolish(spokenHuman.text,userPrompt,true),usage=summarizeGeminiUsageFromTrace(flowTrace),totalMs=Date.now()-started;
  // Un function_call no recibe function_result porque cerramos localmente para ahorrar una segunda IA.
  // Por eso NO encadenamos ese Interaction incompleto: el siguiente turno recibe los últimos turnos visibles como puente local compacto.
  const nextInteractionId=hadTools?'':(payloadId||currentPrev),resetInteractionId=hadTools;
  zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'VNEXT P1.1 · LATENCIA','OK',`total=${totalMs} ms · IA=${modelMs} ms · datos=${toolMs} ms · llamadas IA=${calls} · contratos=${functionCalls.length} · tokens=${num(usage?.totalTokens)} · coste≈${num(usage?.costEurApprox).toFixed(6)} €.`);
  return{ok:true,rejected:false,title:trim(final.title)||'Zuzu VNext',answer,spokenAnswer,warnings:arr(final.warnings),charts:[],tables:tables.filter(Boolean).slice(0,8),files:[],provider:'zuzu-vnext-p11-native-tool-fast-close',model,interactionId:nextInteractionId,conversationId:trim(conversationId),meta:{generatedAt:new Date().toISOString(),version:'v5.2_exp',architecture:'VNext P1.1 · open-world · native function decision · typed business contracts · one-Interaction local close',experimental:true,voiceConversation:!!voiceConversation,interactionId:nextInteractionId,resetInteractionId,spokenAnswer,performance:{totalMs,decisionModelMs:modelMs,dataMs:toolMs,interactionCalls:calls,contractCalls:functionCalls.length},geminiUsageEstimate:usage,debugTrace:arr(flowTrace).slice(0,120)},debugTrace:arr(flowTrace).slice(0,120),showDebugTrace:true};
}


export async function runZuzuP2RPlannerOnly(input={}){
  const userPrompt=trim(input?.prompt);if(!userPrompt){const e=new Error('Escribe una petición para probar el planificador.');e.status=400;throw e;}
  const flowTrace=[],history=arr(input?.conversationHistory).slice(-12),model=(configuredGeminiModelsForTask('zuzu-structured')[0]||'gemini-2.5-flash-lite');
  let screenEventName='';
  if(trim(input?.selectedEventId)){
    const state=input?.stateOverride&&typeof input.stateOverride==='object'?input.stateOverride:await getState({parallel:true});
    screenEventName=trim(v26EventById(state,input.selectedEventId)?.titulo);
  }
  const started=Date.now(),systemInstruction=vnextP2SystemInstruction(input?.selectedEventId,{usuarioLogado:input?.usuarioLogado,user:input?.user,authUser:input?.authUser,ce_acceso:input?.ce_acceso,voiceConversation:false,clientLocalDateTime:input?.clientLocalDateTime,screenEventName});
  const payload=await v261CallInteraction({input:vnextP2Input(userPrompt,history,screenEventName),previousInteractionId:'',model,systemInstruction,tools:vnextP2Tools(),flowTrace,stage:'ITV P2-R · PLANIFICADOR AISLADO',toolChoice:'required',externalSignal:input?.externalSignal,maxCalls:1,maxOutputTokens:360,minOutputTokens:96,plainTextResponse:true});
  const rawCalls=v261FunctionCalls(payload),call=rawCalls.find(x=>trim(x?.name)==='plan_turn')||rawCalls[0]||null,plan=(call?.arguments&&typeof call.arguments==='object')?{...call.arguments}:{},translated=vnextP2RPlanToCalls(plan,history,flowTrace),usage=summarizeGeminiUsageFromTrace(flowTrace);
  return{ok:!!call,plan,translatedCalls:translated.map(x=>({name:trim(x?.name),arguments:x?.arguments&&typeof x.arguments==='object'?x.arguments:{}})),provider:'zuzu-vnext-p2r-planner-only',model,answer:call?`${trim(plan.intent)||'—'} · ${trim(plan.operation)||'—'}`:'Sin plan_turn',meta:{architecture:'VNext P2-R · planner-only · sin ejecución CE',performance:{totalMs:Date.now()-started,decisionModelMs:Date.now()-started,dataMs:0,narrationModelMs:0,interactionCalls:1,decisionCalls:1,narrationCalls:0,contractCalls:0},geminiUsageEstimate:usage,tools:call?['plan_turn']:[],plannerOnly:true,plannerPlan:plan,translatedCalls:translated,debugTrace:arr(flowTrace).slice(0,80)}};
}


// ============================================================================
// ControlEvent v6_1-exp · CLEAN CANONICAL COGNITIVE COMPILER
// Active runtime below intentionally does NOT call any v4/v5 semantic planner,
// prompt classifier, business-intent router, or conversational fallback.
// Legacy helpers may remain in this source tree for historical regressions, but
// runZuzuVNextUserTurn has exactly one cognitive path: Gemini v6.
// ============================================================================

const V6_ONTOLOGY_VERSION='CE-ONTOLOGY-6_1-exp';

function v6SystemPrompt(){return `# SYSTEM CONTEXT: CONTROLEVENT CANONICAL COGNITIVE COMPILER (V6_1-exp · STEP 1)
Eres Zuzu, el asistente cognitivo de ControlEvent. Eres el único componente que interpreta lenguaje natural. CE NO interpreta lenguaje: solo valida el contrato, ejecuta mecánicamente capacidades ya implementadas y devuelve hechos certificados.

STATELESS Y GROUNDING:
- Solo son hechos utilizables los presentes en meta, actor, threads_graph, evidence_ledger.key_facts, recent_dialogue, resolved_materials y user_prompt.
- semantic_summary sirve solo para localizar evidencia; NO autoriza detalles factuales.
- PROHIBIDO inventar IDs, nombres, importes, fechas, estados, cálculos, expectativas, comparaciones, ROI o conclusiones que no estén explícitamente demostradas por key_facts o resolved_materials.
- Cuando emitas una respuesta factual, assistant_response.provenance_evidence_ids debe listar las evidencias del Context Packet que respaldan los hechos usados. Si no hay evidencia factual aplicable (p. ej. saludo/identidad), usa [].
- Si los hechos disponibles no bastan para demostrar algo, dilo de forma natural y transparente. No rellenes huecos con inferencias presentadas como hechos.

IDENTIDAD Y SESIÓN:
- Tu identidad es meta.assistant_identity. Si preguntan quién eres, responde como Zuzu de ControlEvent.
- actor describe al usuario actual. Si actor.name existe y preguntan quién es, úsalo.
- meta.timestamp y meta.timezone son autoritativos para fecha/hora. meta.local_date y meta.local_time son la fecha y hora locales ya materializadas mecánicamente por CE; si preguntan fecha u hora, respóndelas directamente y NUNCA niegues acceso a ellas.
- meta.current_screen_event es el evento visible seleccionado por CE. Cuando el lenguaje del usuario refiera semánticamente al evento visible/actual, usa ese objeto; no pidas al usuario su ID si ya está ahí.

IDIOMA: meta.language es autoritativo para reply, salvo traducción puntual explícita.

THREADS Y CONVERSACIÓN:
- Usa MAINTAIN, SWITCH, OPEN_NEW o CLOSE.
- Mantén continuidad con threads_graph, last_evidence_id y recent_dialogue. No trates cada turno como aislado.
- Confirmaciones, correcciones, continuaciones y referencias deben resolverse semánticamente usando el contexto disponible.
- Si el usuario pide continuar algo ya inferible, continúa; no respondas solo con una promesa.
- Si cambia claramente de tema, conserva el hilo anterior; si pide volver y existe un hilo suficiente, usa SWITCH.
- No pidas repetir información que ya exista en el Context Packet.

ESTADOS DISPONIBLES EN V6_1-exp:
1. NO_ACTION_REQUIRED: ya puedes responder con hechos certificados/contexto suficiente. reply string, render true.
2. EXECUTE_UI: solo cambia representación de una evidencia existente. Pipeline exclusivamente SORT_LIMIT/VISUALIZE. reply string, render true. Como CE ejecuta el cambio DESPUÉS de tu decisión, reply debe ser solo un acuse breve de una frase y NO puede enumerar filas, fechas, importes ni valores resultantes que aún no has visto.
3. EXECUTE_DATA: necesita datos nuevos. Pipeline obligatorio; reply null, render false.
4. NEED_MATERIALS: una evidencia ya listada existe pero key_facts no bastan. required_materials obligatorio; reply null.

CAPACIDADES DE EJECUCIÓN REALES DE ESTE PASO:
- SOLO están disponibles FETCH, FILTER, SORT_LIMIT y VISUALIZE.
- Si una evidencia previa anuncia schema.available_fields y la petición solo reordena o cambia la vista usando uno de esos campos, usa EXECUTE_UI sobre esa evidencia en lugar de afirmar que falta el dato.
- Para VISUALIZE de tipo bar_chart, pie_chart, line_chart o kpi_card debes mapear dimension_x y dimension_y a campos reales disponibles en la evidencia; no anuncies una gráfica si no puede materializarse con filas y valores reales.
- NO existen todavía JOIN, AGGREGATE, DERIVE ni resolución avanzada de entidades. SORT_LIMIT SÍ está disponible y es la primitiva autorizada para ordenar registros por campos permitidos.
- LEY ATÓMICA DE DATOS: si la petición requiere consolidar, cruzar, sumar, agrupar o derivar información y el contrato actual no expone una primitiva capaz de hacerlo ni un campo virtual CE_INTERNAL ya precalculado, está PROHIBIDO intentar obtener el resultado extrayendo filas individuales para calcularlo tú.
- LEY DE INSUFICIENCIA ONTOLÓGICA: si la petición necesita JOIN, AGGREGATE o DERIVE y esas primitivas no existen en este contrato, la única salida válida es NO_ACTION_REQUIRED con una explicación breve y natural de la limitación. No hagas FETCH preparatorio.
- PROHIBICIÓN DE VOLCADO: FETCH solo sirve para recuperar campos atómicos que el usuario desea leer, filtrar, ordenar o visualizar. No lo uses como sustituto de JOIN/AGGREGATE/DERIVE ni solicites colecciones masivas para calcular después en texto.
- Antes de elegir estado/pipeline debes declarar capability_assessment.required_operation con la operación semántica de mayor nivel realmente necesaria: NONE, FETCH, FILTER, SORT_LIMIT, VISUALIZE, JOIN, AGGREGATE o DERIVE. Marca supported=true solo para NONE/FETCH/FILTER/SORT_LIMIT/VISUALIZE. Si declaras JOIN/AGGREGATE/DERIVE, supported=false y la única salida válida es NO_ACTION_REQUIRED, sin FETCH preparatorio.
- La síntesis solo verbaliza campos ya materializados por CE. Está PROHIBIDO cruzar entidades, sumar/agrupar múltiples filas, calcular ratios/porcentajes/ROI o derivar nuevas métricas durante la síntesis.
- Nunca afirmes que una operación se ha realizado si no aparece realmente en el pipeline ejecutado por CE. Que resolved_materials contenga filas no significa que JOIN/AGGREGATE/DERIVE hayan ocurrido.

PRIMITIVAS Y FIRMAS EXACTAS:
- FETCH: {entity_name, projections}. El response_schema limita entidades y campos válidos.
- FILTER: {logical_operator:"AND"|"OR", conditions:[{field,operator,value}]}. El response_schema limita campos y operadores.
- SORT_LIMIT: {sort_by:[{field,direction}], limit, offset}.
- VISUALIZE: {view_type,title,mappings}.

REGLAS DE PIPELINE:
- EXECUTE_DATA debe comenzar por FETCH.
- Usa exclusivamente valores permitidos por response_schema. No inventes campos.
- meta.current_screen_event identifica el evento visible, pero no contiene necesariamente todos sus datos de negocio. Si necesitas datos sustantivos, consulta events y filtra por el id visible.
- En meta.runtime_phase=SYNTHESIS, resolved_materials contiene el resultado certificado de CE: debes cerrar con NO_ACTION_REQUIRED y no pedir otra ejecución.

RESPUESTA FINAL:
- Natural, directa y rigurosa.
- No expongas IDs técnicos, evidencias, primitivas o contratos salvo petición explícita.
- Distingue HECHO CERTIFICADO de CONCLUSIÓN. Una conclusión cualitativa solo puede afirmarse si el Context Packet contiene explícitamente el criterio necesario para sostenerla. Un saldo positivo, una cifra de asistencia o un estado «Finalizado» por sí solos NO autorizan a afirmar «éxito», «cumplió expectativas», «sin riesgos», «rentable» o equivalentes. Si falta el criterio de comparación, objetivo, presupuesto, umbral o expectativa pertinente, dilo expresamente y limita la respuesta a los hechos certificados.
- No conviertas una inferencia en hecho ni inventes cálculos derivados.
- Nunca digas “hecho”, “continuaré” o “aquí tienes” sin entregar el contenido cuando el turno exige contenido.

SALIDA: devuelve única y exclusivamente JSON conforme al response_schema nativo. Sin markdown, sin texto exterior y sin function calling.`;}
function v6Ontology(){return{
  ontology_version:V6_ONTOLOGY_VERSION,
  entities:{
    events:{fields:['id','name','description','status','start_date','end_date','price','canonical_attendance_count','operating_balance','event_valuation']},
    people:{fields:['id','name','rank']},
    purchases:{fields:['id','event_id','product_id','store_id','responsible_id','ticket','units','unit_price','amount','status']},
    incomes:{fields:['id','event_id','person_id','amount','status','concept']},
    donations:{fields:['id','event_id','product_id','donor_id','units','unit_price','estimated_value']},
    stores:{fields:['id','name']}, products:{fields:['id','name','segment','destination','default_price']},
    documents:{fields:['id','event_id','date','text','type']},
    bank_movements:{fields:['id','event_id','date','concept','amount','status']},
    milestones:{fields:['id','event_id','title','description','responsible_id','responsible_name','start_date','end_date','status']},
    tasks:{fields:['id','event_id','milestone_id','description','responsible_id','responsible_name','start_date','end_date','status','notes']},
    attendance:{fields:['event_id','canonical_attendance_count','member_attendance_count','nonmember_attendance_count']}
  },
  relationships:[
    {relationship_id:'event_purchases',from:'events',to:'purchases'}, {relationship_id:'event_incomes',from:'events',to:'incomes'},
    {relationship_id:'purchase_buyer',from:'purchases',to:'people'}, {relationship_id:'purchase_store',from:'purchases',to:'stores'},
    {relationship_id:'purchase_product',from:'purchases',to:'products'}, {relationship_id:'income_payer',from:'incomes',to:'people'},
    {relationship_id:'event_milestones',from:'events',to:'milestones'}, {relationship_id:'milestone_tasks',from:'milestones',to:'tasks'},
    {relationship_id:'event_attendance',from:'events',to:'attendance'}
  ],
  runtime_capabilities:{join_enabled:false},
  canonical_metrics:[
    {metric_id:'canonical_attendance_count',entity:'events',calculation:'CE_INTERNAL'},
    {metric_id:'operating_balance',entity:'events',calculation:'CE_INTERNAL'},
    {metric_id:'event_valuation',entity:'events',calculation:'CE_INTERNAL'}
  ]
};}

function v6ThreadMutationSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['action'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{action:{type:'string',enum:['MAINTAIN','SWITCH','OPEN_NEW','CLOSE']},target_thread_id:{type:['string','null']},updated_goal:{type:'string'},focus_entities:{type:'array',items:{type:'object',required:['id','type'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{id:{type:'string'},type:{type:'string'}}}}}};}
function v6ConditionSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['field','operator'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{field:{type:'string',minLength:1},operator:{type:'string',enum:['EQUALS','NOT_EQUALS','GT','GTE','LT','LTE','IN','CONTAINS','IS_NULL','IS_NOT_NULL']},value:{}}};}
function v6FilterToolSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['logical_operator','conditions'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{logical_operator:{type:'string',enum:['AND','OR']},conditions:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:v6ConditionSchema()}}};}
function v6JoinToolSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['relationship_id'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{relationship_id:{type:'string',minLength:1}}};}
function v6AggregateToolSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['group_by','metrics'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{group_by:{type:'array',items:{type:'string',minLength:1}},metrics:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:{type:'object',required:['field','function','alias'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{field:{type:'string'},function:{type:'string',enum:['COUNT','SUM','AVG','MIN','MAX','STDDEV']},alias:{type:'string',minLength:1}}}}}};}
function v6DeriveToolSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['alias','algebraic_operator','arguments'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{alias:{type:'string',minLength:1},algebraic_operator:{type:'string',enum:['DIFFERENCE','SUM','RATIO','PERCENTAGE','MULTIPLY','DIVIDE']},arguments:{type:'array',minItems:2,maxItems:2,items:{type:'string',minLength:1}}}};}
function v6SortLimitToolSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['sort_by','limit','offset'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{sort_by:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:{type:'object',required:['field','direction'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{field:{type:'string',minLength:1},direction:{type:'string',enum:['ASC','DESC']}}}},limit:{type:'integer',minimum:1,maximum:500},offset:{type:'integer',minimum:0}}};}
function v6VisualizeToolSchema(){return {type:'object',required:['view_type','title','mappings'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{view_type:{type:'string',enum:['table','bar_chart','pie_chart','line_chart','kpi_card','timeline','kanban']},title:{type:'string',minLength:1},mappings:{type:'object',additionalProperties:false,properties:{dimension_x:{type:'string'},dimension_y:{type:'string'},series:{type:'string'},status_field:{type:'string'}}}}};}
function v6FetchToolSchema(){
 const ontology=v6Ontology(), entityNames=Object.keys(ontology.entities||{}), allFields=[...new Set(entityNames.flatMap(k=>arr(ontology.entities?.[k]?.fields)))];
 return {type:'object',required:['entity_name','projections'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{entity_name:{type:'string',enum:entityNames},projections:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:{type:'string',enum:allFields}}}};
}
function v61OutputContractSchema(){
 const focusTypes=['events','people','purchases','incomes','donations'];
 const tm={type:'object',required:['action'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{action:{type:'string',enum:['MAINTAIN','SWITCH','OPEN_NEW','CLOSE']},target_thread_id:{type:['string','null']},updated_goal:{type:'string'},focus_entities:{type:'array',items:{type:'object',required:['id','type'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{id:{type:'string'},type:{type:'string',enum:focusTypes}}}}}};
 const fetchFields={
   events:['id','name','description','status','start_date','end_date','price','canonical_attendance_count','operating_balance','event_valuation'],
   people:['id','name'],
   purchases:['id','amount','status'],
   incomes:['id','amount','status'],
   donations:['id','estimated_value']
 };
 const fetchVariants=Object.entries(fetchFields).map(([entity,fields])=>({type:'object',required:['primitive','params'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{primitive:{type:'string',enum:['FETCH']},params:{type:'object',required:['entity_name','projections'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{entity_name:{type:'string',enum:[entity]},projections:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:{type:'string',enum:fields}}}}}}));
 const filterValue={anyOf:[{type:'string'},{type:'number'},{type:'boolean'},{type:'null'}]};
 const filterNode={type:'object',required:['primitive','params'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{primitive:{type:'string',enum:['FILTER']},params:{type:'object',required:['logical_operator','conditions'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{logical_operator:{type:'string',enum:['AND','OR']},conditions:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:{type:'object',required:['field','operator','value'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{field:{type:'string',enum:['id','name','status']},operator:{type:'string',enum:['EQUALS','NOT_EQUALS','GT','LT','CONTAINS','IS_NULL']},value:filterValue}}}}}}};
 const sortNode={type:'object',required:['primitive','params'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{primitive:{type:'string',enum:['SORT_LIMIT']},params:{type:'object',required:['sort_by','limit','offset'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{sort_by:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:{type:'object',required:['field','direction'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{field:{type:'string',enum:['id','name','start_date','price','amount']},direction:{type:'string',enum:['ASC','DESC']}}}},limit:{type:'integer',minimum:1,maximum:500},offset:{type:'integer',minimum:0}}}}};
 const visualizeTable={type:'object',required:['primitive','params'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{primitive:{type:'string',enum:['VISUALIZE']},params:{type:'object',required:['view_type','title','mappings'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{view_type:{type:'string',enum:['table']},title:{type:'string'},mappings:{type:'object',required:['dimension_x'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{dimension_x:{type:'string'}}}}}}};
 const visualizeChart={type:'object',required:['primitive','params'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{primitive:{type:'string',enum:['VISUALIZE']},params:{type:'object',required:['view_type','title','mappings'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{view_type:{type:'string',enum:['bar_chart','pie_chart','line_chart','kpi_card']},title:{type:'string'},mappings:{type:'object',required:['dimension_x','dimension_y'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{dimension_x:{type:'string'},dimension_y:{type:'string'}}}}}}};
 const visualizeNode={anyOf:[visualizeTable,visualizeChart]};
 const dataPipeline={type:'array',minItems:1,items:{anyOf:[...fetchVariants,filterNode,sortNode,visualizeNode]}};
 const uiPipeline={type:'array',minItems:1,items:{anyOf:[sortNode,visualizeNode]}};
 const provenance={type:'array',items:{type:'string'}};
 const capability={type:'object',required:['required_operation','supported'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{required_operation:{type:'string',enum:['NONE','FETCH','FILTER','SORT_LIMIT','VISUALIZE','JOIN','AGGREGATE','DERIVE']},supported:{type:'boolean'}}};
 const reply=(render,nullable=false)=>({type:'object',required:['should_render_now','provenance_evidence_ids','reply'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{should_render_now:{type:'boolean',enum:[render]},provenance_evidence_ids:provenance,reply:nullable?{type:'null'}:{type:'string'}}});
 const uiReply={type:'object',required:['should_render_now','provenance_evidence_ids','reply'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{should_render_now:{type:'boolean',enum:[true]},provenance_evidence_ids:provenance,reply:{type:'string',maxLength:240,description:'Una sola frase breve de acuse. No enumeres filas ni valores porque CE ejecuta SORT_LIMIT/VISUALIZE después de esta decisión.'}}};
 const noAction={type:'object',required:['capability_assessment','thread_mutation','execution_intent','required_materials','assistant_response'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{capability_assessment:capability,thread_mutation:tm,execution_intent:{type:'object',required:['status'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{status:{type:'string',enum:['NO_ACTION_REQUIRED']}}},required_materials:{type:'array',maxItems:0,items:{type:'string'}},assistant_response:reply(true,false)}};
 const executeUi={type:'object',required:['capability_assessment','thread_mutation','execution_intent','required_materials','assistant_response'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{capability_assessment:capability,thread_mutation:tm,execution_intent:{type:'object',required:['status','source_evidence_id','pipeline'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{status:{type:'string',enum:['EXECUTE_UI']},source_evidence_id:{type:'string'},pipeline:uiPipeline}},required_materials:{type:'array',maxItems:0,items:{type:'string'}},assistant_response:uiReply}};
 const executeData={type:'object',required:['capability_assessment','thread_mutation','execution_intent','required_materials','assistant_response'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{capability_assessment:capability,thread_mutation:tm,execution_intent:{type:'object',required:['status','pipeline'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{status:{type:'string',enum:['EXECUTE_DATA']},pipeline:dataPipeline}},required_materials:{type:'array',maxItems:0,items:{type:'string'}},assistant_response:reply(false,true)}};
 const needMaterials={type:'object',required:['capability_assessment','thread_mutation','execution_intent','required_materials','assistant_response'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{capability_assessment:capability,thread_mutation:tm,execution_intent:{type:'object',required:['status'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{status:{type:'string',enum:['NEED_MATERIALS']}}},required_materials:{type:'array',minItems:1,items:{type:'string'}},assistant_response:reply(false,true)}};
 return {anyOf:[noAction,executeUi,executeData,needMaterials]};
}
function v61SynthesisContractSchema(){
 // La segunda llamada solo verbaliza el resultado ya materializado por CE.
 // La decisión de hilo pertenece exclusivamente a la primera llamada.
 const tm={type:'object',required:['action'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{action:{type:'string',enum:['MAINTAIN']}}};
 return {type:'object',required:['thread_mutation','execution_intent','required_materials','assistant_response'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{
  thread_mutation:tm,
  execution_intent:{type:'object',required:['status'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{status:{type:'string',enum:['NO_ACTION_REQUIRED']}}},
  required_materials:{type:'array',maxItems:0,items:{type:'string'}},
  assistant_response:{type:'object',required:['should_render_now','provenance_evidence_ids','reply'],additionalProperties:false,properties:{should_render_now:{type:'boolean',enum:[true]},provenance_evidence_ids:{type:'array',items:{type:'string'}},reply:{type:'string'}}}
 }};
}
function v61DecodeStructuredContract(payload){
 const raw=v261OutputText(payload);if(!raw)throw new Error('Gemini no devolvió salida JSON estructurada v6_1-exp.');
 let parsed;try{parsed=JSON.parse(raw);}catch(error){throw new Error(`Structured Output v6_1-exp no es JSON válido: ${cleanGeminiError(error)}`);}
 return v6ValidateContract(parsed);
}

function v6StateTools(){throw new Error('Function calling está deshabilitado en v6_1-exp; el único contrato activo es Structured Outputs nativo.');}
function v6CanonicalContractFromToolCall(){throw new Error('Conversión desde function calling deshabilitada en v6_1-exp.');}

function v6ValidatePipeline(pipe=[]){
 const allowedFetch={events:new Set(['id','name','description','status','start_date','end_date','price','canonical_attendance_count','operating_balance','event_valuation']),people:new Set(['id','name']),purchases:new Set(['id','amount','status']),incomes:new Set(['id','amount','status']),donations:new Set(['id','amount','status'])};
 const filterFields=new Set(['id','name','status']),filterOps=new Set(['EQUALS','NOT_EQUALS','GT','LT','CONTAINS','IS_NULL']),sortFields=new Set(['id','name','start_date','price','amount']);
 if(!arr(pipe).length)throw new Error('pipeline vacío.');
 if(trim(pipe[0]?.primitive)!=='FETCH')throw new Error('EXECUTE_DATA debe comenzar con FETCH.');
 let entity='';
 for(const node of arr(pipe)){
   const prim=trim(node?.primitive),p=node?.params&&typeof node.params==='object'?node.params:{};
   if(prim==='FETCH'){
     entity=trim(p.entity_name);const allowed=allowedFetch[entity];if(!allowed)throw new Error(`FETCH entidad no disponible en v6_1-exp: ${entity||'vacía'}.`);
     const projections=arr(p.projections).map(trim).filter(Boolean);if(!projections.length)throw new Error(`FETCH ${entity} requiere projections no vacío.`);
     const bad=projections.filter(f=>!allowed.has(f));if(bad.length)throw new Error(`FETCH ${entity} contiene projection fuera del contrato v6_1-exp: ${bad.join(', ')}.`);
   }else if(prim==='FILTER'){
     if(!['AND','OR'].includes(trim(p.logical_operator)))throw new Error('FILTER requiere logical_operator AND u OR.');
     const cs=arr(p.conditions);if(!cs.length)throw new Error('FILTER requiere al menos una condition.');
     for(const c of cs){const field=trim(c?.field),op=trim(c?.operator);if(!filterFields.has(field))throw new Error(`FILTER usa campo no disponible en v6_1-exp: ${field||'vacío'}.`);if(!filterOps.has(op))throw new Error(`FILTER usa operador no disponible en v6_1-exp: ${op||'vacío'}.`);}
   }else if(prim==='SORT_LIMIT'){
     if(!arr(p.sort_by).length)throw new Error('SORT_LIMIT requiere sort_by.');for(const x of arr(p.sort_by)){if(!sortFields.has(trim(x?.field))||!['ASC','DESC'].includes(trim(x?.direction)))throw new Error('SORT_LIMIT contiene campo o dirección no disponibles en v6_1-exp.');}if(!Number.isFinite(Number(p.limit))||Number(p.limit)<1)throw new Error('SORT_LIMIT requiere limit >= 1.');if(!Number.isFinite(Number(p.offset))||Number(p.offset)<0)throw new Error('SORT_LIMIT requiere offset >= 0.');
   }else if(prim==='VISUALIZE'){
     if(!['table','bar_chart','pie_chart','line_chart','kpi_card'].includes(trim(p.view_type)))throw new Error('VISUALIZE requiere view_type disponible en v6_1-exp.');if(!trim(p.title))throw new Error('VISUALIZE requiere title.');if(!p.mappings||typeof p.mappings!=='object'||!trim(p.mappings.dimension_x))throw new Error('VISUALIZE requiere mappings.dimension_x.');if(trim(p.view_type)!=='table'&&!trim(p.mappings.dimension_y))throw new Error('VISUALIZE gráfico requiere mappings.dimension_y.');
   }else throw new Error(`Primitiva no disponible en v6_1-exp: ${prim||'vacía'}.`);
 }
 return pipe;
}
function v6ValidateContract(o){
 if(!o||typeof o!=='object')throw new Error('Contrato v6_1-exp vacío.');const tm=o.thread_mutation||{},ei=o.execution_intent||{},ar=o.assistant_response||{};
 if(!['MAINTAIN','SWITCH','OPEN_NEW','CLOSE'].includes(trim(tm.action)))throw new Error('thread_mutation.action inválido.');
 const st=trim(ei.status);if(!['NO_ACTION_REQUIRED','EXECUTE_UI','EXECUTE_DATA','NEED_MATERIALS'].includes(st))throw new Error('execution_intent.status no disponible en v6_1-exp.');
 const cap=o.capability_assessment;if(cap){const op=trim(cap.required_operation),supportedOps=new Set(['NONE','FETCH','FILTER','SORT_LIMIT','VISUALIZE']),unsupportedOps=new Set(['JOIN','AGGREGATE','DERIVE']);if(!supportedOps.has(op)&&!unsupportedOps.has(op))throw new Error('capability_assessment.required_operation inválida.');if(supportedOps.has(op)&&cap.supported!==true)throw new Error(`La capacidad ${op} está disponible pero capability_assessment.supported no es true.`);if(unsupportedOps.has(op)&&(cap.supported!==false||st!=='NO_ACTION_REQUIRED'))throw new Error(`La capacidad ${op} no está disponible: debe cerrar en NO_ACTION_REQUIRED sin ejecución.`);}
 if(!Array.isArray(ar.provenance_evidence_ids))throw new Error('assistant_response.provenance_evidence_ids es obligatorio.');
 const pipe=arr(ei.pipeline),mats=arr(o.required_materials),reply=ar.reply;
 if(st==='NO_ACTION_REQUIRED'&&(pipe.length||mats.length||reply==null))throw new Error('NO_ACTION_REQUIRED incompatible.');
 if(st==='EXECUTE_UI'){if(!trim(ei.source_evidence_id)||!pipe.length||pipe.some(x=>!['SORT_LIMIT','VISUALIZE'].includes(trim(x?.primitive)))||reply==null)throw new Error('EXECUTE_UI incompatible.');}
 if(st==='EXECUTE_DATA'){if(!pipe.length||reply!==null)throw new Error('EXECUTE_DATA requiere pipeline y reply=null.');v6ValidatePipeline(pipe);}
 if(st==='NEED_MATERIALS'&&(!mats.length||reply!==null||pipe.length))throw new Error('NEED_MATERIALS incompatible.');
 return o;
}
function v6LastContext(history=[]){for(const h of arr(history).slice().reverse()){const rc=h?.resultContext||h?.meta?.resultContext;if(rc?.v6)return rc;}return{};}
function v6LocalClock(isoValue,timeZone='Europe/Madrid'){const raw=trim(isoValue)||new Date().toISOString(),tz=trim(timeZone)||'Europe/Madrid';let d=new Date(raw);if(!Number.isFinite(d.getTime()))d=new Date();try{const parts=Object.fromEntries(new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:tz,year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',second:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23'}).formatToParts(d).filter(x=>x.type!=='literal').map(x=>[x.type,x.value]));return{date:`${parts.year}-${parts.month}-${parts.day}`,time:`${parts.hour}:${parts.minute}`,datetime:`${parts.year}-${parts.month}-${parts.day} ${parts.hour}:${parts.minute}:${parts.second}`};}catch(_){return{date:d.toISOString().slice(0,10),time:d.toISOString().slice(11,16),datetime:d.toISOString().slice(0,19).replace('T',' ')}}}
function v6ContextPacket({userPrompt,history,state,selectedEventId,conversationLanguage,clientNowIso,clientTimeZone,resolvedMaterials={},runtimePhase='DECISION'}){
 const hist=arr(history),prev=v6LastContext(hist),actor=state?.usuarioLogado||state?.ce_acceso_usuario_logado||{};
 const threads=prev?.threads_graph||{active_thread_id:'TH-INIT',threads:[{id:'TH-INIT',status:'ACTIVE',goal:'Inicio',focus_entities:[],last_evidence_id:null,current_view:null}]};
 const ledger=arr(prev?.evidence_ledger).slice(-18).map(e=>({id:e.id,ontology_version:e.ontology_version,schema:e.schema,semantic_summary:e.semantic_summary,key_facts:e.key_facts}));
 const current=selectedEventId?v26EventById(state,selectedEventId):null,last=hist[hist.length-1]||{},lastRc=last?.resultContext||last?.meta?.resultContext||{},lastDecision=lastRc?.last_turn?.decision||last?.meta?.sovereignDecision||{};
 const activeThread=arr(threads?.threads).find(x=>trim(x?.id)===trim(threads?.active_thread_id))||null;
 const recent=hist.slice(-6).flatMap(h=>[{role:'user',text:trim(h?.user).slice(0,420)},{role:'assistant',text:trim(h?.assistant).slice(0,620)}]).filter(x=>x.text);
 const conversationState={
   active_thread_id:trim(threads?.active_thread_id),
   previous_user_prompt:trim(last?.user).slice(0,500),
   previous_assistant_reply:trim(last?.assistant).slice(0,700),
   previous_execution_status:trim(lastDecision?.execution_intent?.status||lastRc?.last_turn?.execution_status),
   previous_thread_action:trim(lastDecision?.thread_mutation?.action||lastRc?.last_turn?.thread_action),
   active_last_evidence_id:trim(activeThread?.last_evidence_id),
   active_goal:trim(activeThread?.goal),
   available_threads:arr(threads?.threads).map(t=>({id:trim(t?.id),status:trim(t?.status),goal:trim(t?.goal),last_evidence_id:trim(t?.last_evidence_id)})).slice(-8)
 };
 const authoritativeTimestamp=trim(clientNowIso)||new Date().toISOString(),authoritativeTimezone=trim(clientTimeZone)||'Europe/Madrid',localClock=v6LocalClock(authoritativeTimestamp,authoritativeTimezone);
 return {meta:{ontology_version:V6_ONTOLOGY_VERSION,runtime_phase:trim(runtimePhase)||'DECISION',assistant_identity:{name:'Zuzu',product:'ControlEvent'},timestamp:authoritativeTimestamp,timezone:authoritativeTimezone,local_date:localClock.date,local_time:localClock.time,local_datetime:localClock.datetime,language:trim(conversationLanguage)||'es-ES',current_screen_event:current?{id:trim(current.id),name:trim(current.titulo||current.nombre)}:null},actor:{id:trim(actor?.id||actor?.user_id),role:trim(actor?.rol||actor?.role),name:trim(actor?.nombre||actor?.name)},conversation_state:conversationState,threads_graph:threads,evidence_ledger:ledger,recent_dialogue:recent,resolved_materials:resolvedMaterials,user_prompt:userPrompt,ontology:v6Ontology()};
}
function v6Norm(x){return norm(trim(x));}
function v6ResolveOne(state,textValue,targetTypes=[]){
 const q=v6Norm(textValue);if(!q)return{status:'none',candidates:[]};const out=[];const add=(type,id,name)=>{const n=v6Norm(name);if(!n)return;if(n===q||n.includes(q)||q.includes(n))out.push({type,id:trim(id),name:trim(name),score:n===q?1:.8});};
 for(const t of arr(targetTypes)){if(t==='events')arr(state?.eventos).forEach(x=>add('events',x?.id,x?.titulo||x?.nombre));if(t==='people')arr(state?.personas).forEach(x=>add('people',x?.id,x?.nombre));if(t==='products')arr(state?.productos).forEach(x=>add('products',x?.id,x?.nombre));if(t==='stores')arr(state?.tiendas).forEach(x=>add('stores',x?.id,x?.nombre));}
 out.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||a.name.localeCompare(b.name,'es'));const top=out.filter(x=>x.score===out[0]?.score);return top.length===1?{status:'resolved',candidate:top[0],candidates:top.slice(0,8)}:{status:top.length?'ambiguous':'none',candidates:top.slice(0,8)};
}
function v6Rows(state,name){
 const entity=trim(name),events=byId(state?.eventos),people=byId(state?.personas),products=byId(state?.productos),stores=byId(state?.tiendas);
 if(entity==='events'){const cs=compactState(state,''),sum=new Map(arr(cs?.eventosResumen).map(x=>[trim(x?.id),x])),att=buildCanonicalAttendance(state,arr(state?.eventos).map(e=>trim(e?.id)).filter(Boolean)),am=new Map(arr(att?.porEvento).map(x=>[trim(x?.eventId),x]));return arr(state?.eventos).map(e=>{const id=trim(e?.id),m=sum.get(id)||{},a=am.get(id)||{};return{id,name:trim(e?.titulo||e?.nombre),description:trim(e?.descripcion),status:trim(e?.situacion),start_date:trim(e?.fechaIni),end_date:trim(e?.fechaFin),price:num(e?.precio),canonical_attendance_count:num(a?.totalAsistentesPersonas??a?.total??a?.count),operating_balance:round(num(m?.ingresosTotal)-num(m?.comprasReales)-num(m?.comprasPendientes),2),event_valuation:round(num(m?.comprasReales)+num(m?.comprasPendientes)+num(m?.donacionesValor),2)};});}
 if(entity==='people')return arr(state?.personas).map(p=>({id:trim(p?.id),name:trim(p?.nombre),rank:trim(p?.rango)}));
 if(entity==='stores')return arr(state?.tiendas).map(x=>({id:trim(x?.id),name:trim(x?.nombre)}));
 if(entity==='products')return arr(state?.productos).map(x=>({id:trim(x?.id),name:trim(x?.nombre),segment:trim(x?.segmento||x?.segment),destination:trim(x?.destino||x?.destination),default_price:num(x?.defaultPrecio??x?.precio)}));
 if(entity==='purchases')return arr(state?.compras).filter(x=>!isDonationTicket(ticketText(x))).map((x,i)=>({id:trim(x?.id)||`PUR-${i+1}`,event_id:trim(x?.eventId||x?.event_id),product_id:trim(x?.productoId||x?.producto_id),store_id:trim(x?.tiendaId||x?.tienda_id),responsible_id:trim(x?.responsableId||x?.responsable_id),ticket:ticketText(x),units:num(x?.unidades??x?.cantidad??x?.uds),unit_price:num(x?.precio),amount:valueOfLine(x),status:isPendingTicket(ticketText(x))?'PENDING':'REALIZED'}));
 if(entity==='donations')return arr(state?.compras).filter(x=>isDonationTicket(ticketText(x))).map((x,i)=>({id:trim(x?.id)||`DON-${i+1}`,event_id:trim(x?.eventId||x?.event_id),product_id:trim(x?.productoId||x?.producto_id),donor_id:trim(text(x?.donorRef||x?.donor_ref).replace(/^P:/,'')),units:num(x?.unidades??x?.cantidad??x?.uds),unit_price:num(x?.precio),estimated_value:valueOfLine(x)}));
 if(entity==='incomes'){const out=[];for(const e of arr(state?.eventos)){const eid=trim(e?.id),cs=compactState(state,eid),rows=arr(cs?.detalleEventoSeleccionado?.ingresos),raw=arr(state?.colaboradores).filter(x=>trim(x?.eventId||x?.event_id)===eid);rows.forEach((r,i)=>{const x=raw[i]||{};out.push({id:trim(x?.id)||`INC-${out.length+1}`,event_id:eid,person_id:trim(x?.personaId||x?.persona_id||x?.colaboradorId||x?.colaborador_id),amount:num(r?.importeTotalCalculado),status:trim(r?.formaPago),concept:trim(x?.concepto||x?.concept||r?.tipoPersona)});});}return out;}
 if(entity==='bank_movements')return arr(state?.bankMovements).map((x,i)=>({id:trim(x?.id)||`BANK-${i+1}`,event_id:trim(x?.eventId||x?.event_id),date:trim(x?.fecha||x?.date||x?.value_date),concept:trim(x?.concepto||x?.concept),amount:num(x?.importe??x?.amount),status:trim(x?.situacion||x?.status)}));
 if(entity==='milestones')return arr(state?.hitos).map((x,i)=>({id:trim(x?.id)||`H-${i+1}`,event_id:trim(x?.eventId||x?.event_id),title:trim(x?.nombreHito||x?.titulo||x?.descripcion),description:trim(x?.descripcion),responsible_id:trim(x?.responsableId||x?.responsable_id),responsible_name:trim(x?.responsableNombre||x?.responsable_nombre),start_date:trim(x?.fechaInicio||x?.fecha_inicio),end_date:trim(x?.fechaFin||x?.fecha_fin),status:x?.cumplida===true?'COMPLETED':trim(x?.estado||x?.status)||'PENDING'}));
 if(entity==='tasks')return arr(state?.lgs).map((x,i)=>({id:trim(x?.id)||`LG-${i+1}`,event_id:trim(x?.eventId||x?.event_id),milestone_id:trim(x?.hitoId||x?.hito_id),description:trim(x?.descripcion),responsible_id:trim(x?.responsableId||x?.responsable_id),responsible_name:trim(x?.responsableNombre||x?.responsable_nombre),start_date:trim(x?.fechaInicio||x?.fecha_inicio),end_date:trim(x?.fechaFin||x?.fecha_fin),status:x?.cumplida===true?'COMPLETED':'PENDING',notes:trim(x?.notas||x?.notes)}));
 if(entity==='documents')return arr(state?.documentos||state?.documents).map((x,i)=>({id:trim(x?.id)||`DOC-${i+1}`,event_id:trim(x?.eventId||x?.event_id),date:trim(x?.fecha||x?.date),text:trim(x?.texto||x?.text||x?.descripcion),type:trim(x?.tipo||x?.type)}));
 if(entity==='attendance'){const ids=arr(state?.eventos).map(e=>trim(e?.id)).filter(Boolean),a=buildCanonicalAttendance(state,ids);return arr(a?.porEvento).map(x=>({event_id:trim(x?.eventId),canonical_attendance_count:num(x?.asistentes??x?.total??x?.count),member_attendance_count:num(x?.socios??x?.sociosAsistentes),nonmember_attendance_count:num(x?.noSocios??x?.noSociosAsistentes)}));}
 return[];
}
function v6Filter(rows,p={}){const test=(r,c)=>{const v=r?.[c.field],x=c.value,op=trim(c.operator);if(op==='EQUALS')return String(v??'')===String(x??'');if(op==='NOT_EQUALS')return String(v??'')!==String(x??'');if(op==='GT')return num(v)>num(x);if(op==='GTE')return num(v)>=num(x);if(op==='LT')return num(v)<num(x);if(op==='LTE')return num(v)<=num(x);if(op==='IN')return arr(x).map(String).includes(String(v));if(op==='CONTAINS')return v6Norm(v).includes(v6Norm(x));if(op==='IS_NULL')return v==null||v==='';if(op==='IS_NOT_NULL')return !(v==null||v==='');return false;};const cs=arr(p.conditions);return rows.filter(r=>(trim(p.logical_operator)==='OR'?cs.some(c=>test(r,c)):cs.every(c=>test(r,c))));}
function v6Aggregate(rows,p={}){const groups=new Map(),gb=arr(p.group_by);for(const r of rows){const key=JSON.stringify(gb.map(k=>r?.[k]));if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);groups.get(key).push(r);}const sets=groups.size?[...groups.values()]:[rows];return sets.map(g=>{const o={};gb.forEach(k=>o[k]=g[0]?.[k]);for(const m of arr(p.metrics)){const vals=g.map(r=>num(r?.[m.field]));let v=0;if(m.function==='COUNT')v=g.length;else if(m.function==='SUM')v=vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);else if(m.function==='AVG')v=vals.length?vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/vals.length:0;else if(m.function==='MIN')v=vals.length?Math.min(...vals):0;else if(m.function==='MAX')v=vals.length?Math.max(...vals):0;else if(m.function==='STDDEV'){const av=vals.length?vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/vals.length:0;v=vals.length?Math.sqrt(vals.reduce((s,x)=>s+(x-av)**2,0)/vals.length):0;}o[m.alias]=v;}return o;});}
function v6KeyFacts(rows=[]){const facts={row_count:rows.length};if(rows.length===1){for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(rows[0]||{})){if(Object.keys(facts).length>=7)break;if(['string','number','boolean'].includes(typeof v)||v===null)facts[k]=v;}}return facts;}
function v6Substitute(v,map){if(typeof v==='string'&&map[v])return map[v];if(Array.isArray(v))return v.map(x=>v6Substitute(x,map));if(v&&typeof v==='object')return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).map(([k,x])=>[k,v6Substitute(x,map)]));return v;}
function v6ProjectRows(rows,projections=[]){const p=arr(projections);return arr(rows).map(r=>Object.fromEntries((p.length?p:Object.keys(r||{})).filter(k=>k in (r||{})).map(k=>[k,r[k]])));}
function v6ComparableValue(field,value){
 const f=trim(field),v=value;
 if(/(?:^|_)date$/.test(f)||/(?:^|_)date_/.test(f)||f==='start_date'||f==='end_date'){
   const t=trim(v);
   if(!t)return {kind:'date',value:Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY};
   const dm=t.match(/^(\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{1,2})[\/-](\d{4})(?:\s+.*)?$/);
   let ms=dm?Date.UTC(Number(dm[3]),Number(dm[2])-1,Number(dm[1])):Date.parse(t);
   return {kind:'date',value:Number.isFinite(ms)?ms:Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY};
 }
 if(typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v))return {kind:'number',value:v};
 return {kind:'text',value:String(v??'')};
}
function v6SortRows(rows,p={}){let out=arr(rows).slice();for(const s of arr(p.sort_by).slice().reverse())out.sort((a,b)=>{const av=v6ComparableValue(s.field,a?.[s.field]),bv=v6ComparableValue(s.field,b?.[s.field]);let c=0;if(av.kind===bv.kind&&(av.kind==='date'||av.kind==='number'))c=av.value-bv.value;else c=String(av.value).localeCompare(String(bv.value),'es',{numeric:true});return trim(s.direction)==='DESC'?-c:c;});return out.slice(num(p.offset),num(p.offset)+(num(p.limit)||out.length));}
function v6ExecutePipeline(state,pipeline=[]){let rows=[],backingRows=[],entity='',view=null,projections=[];for(const node of arr(pipeline)){const prim=trim(node?.primitive),p=node?.params||{};if(prim==='FETCH'){entity=trim(p.entity_name);const allowed=v6Ontology().entities?.[entity]?.fields||[];if(!allowed.length)throw new Error(`Entidad lógica no permitida: ${entity}`);projections=arr(p.projections);if(projections.some(f=>!allowed.includes(f)))throw new Error('FETCH contiene campos fuera de ontología.');backingRows=v6Rows(state,entity).map(r=>({...r}));rows=v6ProjectRows(backingRows,projections);}else if(prim==='FILTER'){backingRows=v6Filter(backingRows,p);rows=v6ProjectRows(backingRows,projections);}else if(prim==='SORT_LIMIT'){backingRows=v6SortRows(backingRows,p);rows=v6ProjectRows(backingRows,projections);}else if(prim==='VISUALIZE'){view={...p};}else{throw new Error(`Primitiva no disponible en v6_1-exp: ${prim||'vacía'}.`);}}return{entity,rows,backingRows,projections,view};}
function v6ChartSpec(rows=[],view={}){const type=trim(view?.view_type),m=view?.mappings||{},x=trim(m.dimension_x),y=trim(m.dimension_y);if(!type||type==='table')return null;if(!x||!y)throw new Error('La gráfica necesita dimension_x y dimension_y.');const rr=arr(rows);if(!rr.length)throw new Error('No hay filas para materializar la gráfica.');if(rr.some(r=>!(x in (r||{}))||!(y in (r||{}))))throw new Error(`La gráfica usa campos no disponibles en la evidencia: ${x}/${y}.`);const values=rr.map(r=>Number(r?.[y]));if(values.some(v=>!Number.isFinite(v)))throw new Error(`La dimensión Y de la gráfica no es numérica: ${y}.`);const frontendType=type==='bar_chart'?'bar':type==='pie_chart'?'pie':type==='line_chart'?'line':'bar';const unit=/(?:amount|price|balance|valuation|value|importe|precio|saldo)/i.test(y)?'€':'';return{title:trim(view?.title)||'Gráfica',type:frontendType,labels:rr.map(r=>String(r?.[x]??'')),values,unit,mappings:{dimension_x:x,dimension_y:y}};}

function v6ApplyThread(prevThreads,mutation,evidenceId='',view=null){const g=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(prevThreads||{active_thread_id:'TH-INIT',threads:[]})),a=trim(mutation?.action)||'MAINTAIN';let target=trim(mutation?.target_thread_id),active=g.threads.find(x=>x.id===g.active_thread_id);if(a==='OPEN_NEW'){target=`TH-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;if(active)active.status='SUSPENDED';active={id:target,status:'ACTIVE',goal:trim(mutation?.updated_goal)||'Nuevo hilo',focus_entities:arr(mutation?.focus_entities),last_evidence_id:evidenceId||null,current_view:view||null};g.threads.push(active);g.active_thread_id=target;}else if(a==='SWITCH'){const t=g.threads.find(x=>x.id===target);if(t){g.threads.forEach(x=>x.status=x.id===target?'ACTIVE':'SUSPENDED');g.active_thread_id=target;active=t;if(trim(mutation?.updated_goal))active.goal=trim(mutation.updated_goal);if(arr(mutation?.focus_entities).length)active.focus_entities=arr(mutation.focus_entities);if(evidenceId)active.last_evidence_id=evidenceId;if(view)active.current_view=view;}}else if(a==='CLOSE'){if(active)active.status='CLOSED';}else if(active){if(trim(mutation?.updated_goal))active.goal=trim(mutation.updated_goal);if(arr(mutation?.focus_entities).length)active.focus_entities=arr(mutation.focus_entities);if(evidenceId)active.last_evidence_id=evidenceId;if(view)active.current_view=view;}
 return g;}
function v61JsonSchemaForGemini(schema){
 const allowed=new Set(['$id','$defs','$ref','$anchor','type','format','title','description','enum','items','prefixItems','minItems','maxItems','minLength','maxLength','minimum','maximum','anyOf','oneOf','properties','additionalProperties','required']);
 const walk=(node,key='')=>{
   if(Array.isArray(node))return node.map(x=>walk(x));
   if(!node||typeof node!=='object')return node;
   const out={};
   for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(node)){
     if(key==='properties'){out[k]=walk(v);continue;}
     if(!allowed.has(k))continue;
     if(k==='properties'){out[k]=walk(v,'properties');continue;}
     out[k]=walk(v,k);
   }
   return out;
 };
 return walk(schema);
}
async function v61CallStrictStructuredOutput({packet,flowTrace=[],model,externalSignal=null,stage='V6 · STRUCTURED OUTPUT',mode='DECISION'}={}){
 const apiKey=geminiKey();if(!apiKey){const e=new Error('Falta GEMINI_API_KEY para Zuzu.');e.status=503;throw e;}
 const isSynthesis=mode==='SYNTHESIS';
 const schema=v61JsonSchemaForGemini(isSynthesis?v61SynthesisContractSchema():v61OutputContractSchema());
 const phaseInstruction=isSynthesis?'\n\nFASE DE SÍNTESIS CERTIFICADA: resolved_materials ya contiene EXACTAMENTE los datos ejecutados por CE. Debes cerrar AHORA con NO_ACTION_REQUIRED. No solicites nuevas operaciones, materiales ni cambios de UI. Esta fase es SOLO verbalización: PROHIBIDO unir entidades, sumar/agrupar filas, contar para crear métricas nuevas, calcular ratios/porcentajes/ROI o derivar resultados que no estén ya materializados explícitamente. No afirmes que CE ejecutó una operación que no figure en el pipeline previo. Si resolved_materials contiene DATA_VOLUME_EXCEEDED, explica de forma breve que el volumen supera la lectura directa y que debe acotarse con FILTER o SORT_LIMIT; no inventes resultados. Si un material tiene status=CERTIFIED_DATASET, sus rows son INMUTABLES: conserva EXACTAMENTE la secuencia de rows y cada valor escalar tal como aparece. PROHIBIDO corregir, reinterpretar, traducir o reformatear fechas, números, nombres o estados; por ejemplo, 21/08/2026 debe seguir siendo 21/08/2026. Si schema.order_by no está vacío, la enumeración debe seguir exactamente ese orden físico. Puedes añadir conectores naturales alrededor de los valores, pero nunca reconstruir las filas desde memoria, recent_dialogue o conocimiento propio. Redacta solo lo demostrado por key_facts/resolved_materials y cita sus evidence IDs en provenance_evidence_ids.':'\n\nFASE DE DECISIÓN: completa capability_assessment ANTES de planificar. JOIN/AGGREGATE/DERIVE => supported=false y NO_ACTION_REQUIRED, sin FETCH. NONE/FETCH/FILTER/SORT_LIMIT/VISUALIZE => supported=true. Para preguntas de fecha/hora usa directamente meta.local_date/meta.local_time. En EXECUTE_UI el reply es solo un acuse breve; CE materializa el resultado después.';
 const body={
   systemInstruction:{parts:[{text:v6SystemPrompt()+phaseInstruction}]},
   contents:[{role:'user',parts:[{text:JSON.stringify(packet)}]}],
   generationConfig:{
     responseMimeType:'application/json',
     responseJsonSchema:schema,
     maxOutputTokens:isSynthesis?1000:1200
   }
 };
 const url=`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(model)}:generateContent`;
 const started=Date.now();
 let res,payload;
 try{({res,payload}=await geminiFetchJsonWithTimeout(url,body,apiKey,Number(process.env.CONTROLEVENT_ZUZU_INTERACTIONS_TIMEOUT_MS||45000),externalSignal));}
 catch(error){zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,stage,'KO',cleanGeminiError(error),{model,transport:'generateContent+responseJsonSchema'});throw error;}
 const usage=usageSmall(payload,model);
 if(!res.ok){const e=new Error(payload?.error?.message||`Gemini Structured Output HTTP ${res.status}`);e.status=res.status;e.details=payload;zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,stage,'KO',cleanGeminiError(e),{model,usage,transport:'generateContent+responseJsonSchema',elapsedMs:Date.now()-started});throw e;}
 const raw=geminiOutText(payload);
 zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,stage,'OK',`Structured Output nativo · generateContent + responseJsonSchema · ${Date.now()-started} ms.`,{model,usage,transport:'generateContent+responseJsonSchema'});
 if(!raw)throw new Error('Gemini no devolvió salida JSON estructurada v6_1-exp.');
 let parsed;
 try{parsed=JSON.parse(raw);}catch(error){throw new Error(`Gemini incumplió Structured Output nativo: ${cleanGeminiError(error)}`);}
 return v6ValidateContract(parsed);
}
async function v6GeminiDecision(packet,flowTrace,model,externalSignal,stage,mode='DECISION'){
 return v61CallStrictStructuredOutput({packet,flowTrace,model,externalSignal,stage,mode});
}

async function runZuzuV6Agent({userPrompt,statePromise,selectedEventId,flowTrace=[],conversationHistory=[],voiceConversation=false,usuarioLogado,user,authUser,ce_acceso,clientNowIso,clientLocalDateTime,clientTimeZone,conversationLanguage='es-ES',externalSignal=null,conversationId=''}={}){
 const started=Date.now(),model=(configuredGeminiModelsForTask('zuzu-structured')[0]||'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'),state=attachLoggedUserFix10((await statePromise)||{},{usuarioLogado,user,authUser,ce_acceso});state.usuarioLogado=usuarioLogado||user||authUser||ce_acceso||state.usuarioLogado;
 let packet=v6ContextPacket({userPrompt,history:conversationHistory,state,selectedEventId,conversationLanguage,clientNowIso:clientNowIso||clientLocalDateTime,clientTimeZone,runtimePhase:'DECISION'}),decision=await v6GeminiDecision(packet,flowTrace,model,externalSignal,'V6 · DECISIÓN SOBERANA','DECISION');let calls=1,resolvedMaterials={},table=null,charts=[],evidence=null;
 const prev=v6LastContext(conversationHistory),store={...(prev?.evidence_store||{})},ledger=arr(prev?.evidence_ledger).slice(-17),prevThreads=packet.threads_graph;
 if(decision.execution_intent.status==='NEED_MATERIALS'){for(const id of arr(decision.required_materials)){if(store[id]){const src=store[id];resolvedMaterials[id]={status:'CERTIFIED_DATASET',schema:src.schema||{columns:Object.keys(arr(src.rows)[0]||{}),row_count:arr(src.rows).length,order_by:[]},rows:arr(src.rows)};}}packet=v6ContextPacket({userPrompt,history:conversationHistory,state,selectedEventId,conversationLanguage,clientNowIso:clientNowIso||clientLocalDateTime,clientTimeZone,resolvedMaterials,runtimePhase:'SYNTHESIS'});decision=await v6GeminiDecision(packet,flowTrace,model,externalSignal,'V6 · RESPUESTA CON MATERIALES','SYNTHESIS');calls++;}
 if(decision.execution_intent.status==='EXECUTE_UI'){const id=trim(decision.execution_intent.source_evidence_id),src=store[id];if(!src)throw new Error(`No existe la evidencia ${id} para EXECUTE_UI.`);let backingRows=arr(src.backing_rows).length?arr(src.backing_rows).map(r=>({...r})):arr(src.rows).map(r=>({...r})),view=src.view||null;const visibleColumns=arr(src?.schema?.columns).length?arr(src.schema.columns):Object.keys(src.rows?.[0]||{});for(const n of arr(decision.execution_intent.pipeline)){if(trim(n.primitive)==='SORT_LIMIT')backingRows=v6SortRows(backingRows,n.params);if(trim(n.primitive)==='VISUALIZE')view=n.params;}const rows=v6ProjectRows(backingRows,visibleColumns);table={key:id,title:trim(view?.title)||src.title||'Datos',columns:visibleColumns,rows};const chart=v6ChartSpec(backingRows,view);if(chart)charts=[chart];store[id]={...src,rows,backing_rows:backingRows,view};const threads=v6ApplyThread(prevThreads,decision.thread_mutation,'',view);return v6Finish({answer:decision.assistant_response.reply,model,started,calls,flowTrace,conversationId,conversationLanguage,decision,threads,ledger,store,tables:[table],charts,voiceConversation,state,clientTimeZone,clientNowIso});}
 if(decision.execution_intent.status==='EXECUTE_DATA'){
   const pipe=decision.execution_intent.pipeline,out=v6ExecutePipeline(state,pipe),id=`EV-${Date.now().toString(36).toUpperCase()}`;
   const sortNode=[...arr(pipe)].reverse().find(n=>trim(n?.primitive)==='SORT_LIMIT'),orderBy=arr(sortNode?.params?.sort_by).map(x=>({field:trim(x?.field),direction:trim(x?.direction)})).filter(x=>x.field&&x.direction);
   const directRowLimit=50,overflow=out.rows.length>directRowLimit;
   const schema={columns:Object.keys(out.rows[0]||{}),available_fields:Object.keys(out.backingRows[0]||{}),row_count:out.rows.length,order_by:orderBy};
   evidence={id,ontology_version:V6_ONTOLOGY_VERSION,schema,semantic_summary:overflow?`Volumen directo excedido en ${out.entity||'pipeline lógico'}`:`Resultado certificado de ${out.entity||'pipeline lógico'}`,key_facts:overflow?{row_count:out.rows.length,data_volume_exceeded:true,max_direct_rows:directRowLimit}:v6KeyFacts(out.rows)};
   store[id]={...evidence,entity:out.entity,rows:overflow?[]:out.rows,backing_rows:overflow?[]:out.backingRows,projections:out.projections,view:overflow?null:out.view};ledger.push(evidence);
   if(overflow){resolvedMaterials[id]={status:'DATA_VOLUME_EXCEEDED',total_rows:out.rows.length,max_direct_rows:directRowLimit,suggestion:'Acota el conjunto con FILTER o SORT_LIMIT antes de solicitar lectura directa.'};table={key:id,title:`Resultado · ${out.entity||'datos'}`,columns:['status','total_rows','max_direct_rows'],rows:[resolvedMaterials[id]]};}
   else{resolvedMaterials[id]={status:'CERTIFIED_DATASET',schema:{columns:schema.columns,row_count:schema.row_count,order_by:schema.order_by,scalar_fidelity:'EXACT',row_sequence_fidelity:'EXACT'},rows:out.rows};table={key:id,title:trim(out.view?.title)||`Resultado · ${out.entity||'datos'}`,columns:schema.columns,rows:out.rows};const chart=v6ChartSpec(out.backingRows,out.view);if(chart)charts=[chart];}
   const threads1=v6ApplyThread(prevThreads,decision.thread_mutation,id,overflow?null:out.view);
   packet=v6ContextPacket({userPrompt,history:[...conversationHistory,{user:userPrompt,assistant:'',resultContext:{v6:true,threads_graph:threads1,evidence_ledger:ledger,evidence_store:store}}],state,selectedEventId,conversationLanguage,clientNowIso:clientNowIso||clientLocalDateTime,clientTimeZone,resolvedMaterials,runtimePhase:'SYNTHESIS'});let finalDecision=await v6GeminiDecision(packet,flowTrace,model,externalSignal,'V6 · SÍNTESIS CERTIFICADA','SYNTHESIS');calls++;return v6Finish({answer:finalDecision.assistant_response.reply||'He obtenido los datos, pero Gemini no ha cerrado una respuesta textual válida.',model,started,calls,flowTrace,conversationId,conversationLanguage,decision:finalDecision,threads:v6ApplyThread(threads1,finalDecision.thread_mutation),ledger,store,tables:[table],charts,voiceConversation,state,clientTimeZone,clientNowIso});
 }

 return v6Finish({answer:decision.assistant_response.reply,model,started,calls,flowTrace,conversationId,conversationLanguage,decision,threads:v6ApplyThread(prevThreads,decision.thread_mutation),ledger,store,tables:[],charts:[],voiceConversation,state,clientTimeZone,clientNowIso});
}
function v6Finish({answer='',model,started,calls,flowTrace,conversationId,conversationLanguage,decision,threads,ledger,store,tables=[],charts=[],voiceConversation,state,clientTimeZone,clientNowIso}){
 const textAnswer=trim(answer)||'No puedo cerrar esa respuesta con los datos certificados disponibles.';
 const spoken=voiceConversation?v4BrainLiteVoiceSafe(textAnswer,state,{timeZone:clientTimeZone,nowIso:clientNowIso}):textAnswer;
 const lastTurn={execution_status:trim(decision?.execution_intent?.status),thread_action:trim(decision?.thread_mutation?.action),provenance_evidence_ids:arr(decision?.assistant_response?.provenance_evidence_ids),reply:textAnswer.slice(0,900),decision:{thread_mutation:{action:trim(decision?.thread_mutation?.action),target_thread_id:decision?.thread_mutation?.target_thread_id??null},execution_intent:{status:trim(decision?.execution_intent?.status)}}};
 return{ok:true,rejected:false,title:'Zuzu',answer:textAnswer,spokenAnswer:spoken,warnings:[],charts,tables,files:[],provider:'zuzu-v6_1-exp-structured-output',model,interactionId:'',conversationId:trim(conversationId),meta:{version:'v6_1-exp',architecture:'v6_1-exp · STEP 1 · STRUCTURED OUTPUTS · V6.0.10 BEHAVIOR BASELINE',sovereignDecision:decision,resultContext:{v6:true,threads_graph:threads,evidence_ledger:ledger,evidence_store:store,conversation_language:trim(conversationLanguage)||'es-ES',ontology_version:V6_ONTOLOGY_VERSION,last_turn:lastTurn},tools:decision?.execution_intent?.status==='EXECUTE_DATA'?['v6_pipeline']:decision?.execution_intent?.status==='EXECUTE_UI'?['v6_ui']:[],performance:{totalMs:Date.now()-started,interactionCalls:calls},geminiUsageEstimate:summarizeGeminiUsageFromTrace(flowTrace),debugTrace:arr(flowTrace).slice(0,120)},debugTrace:arr(flowTrace).slice(0,120),showDebugTrace:true};
}

export async function runZuzuVNextUserTurn(input={}){
 const flowTrace=[],userPrompt=trim(input?.prompt);if(!userPrompt){const e=new Error('Escribe una pregunta o petición para Zuzu.');e.status=400;throw e;}if(userPrompt.length>3000){const e=new Error('El prompt es demasiado largo. Resume la petición.');e.status=413;throw e;}
 const statePromise=input?.stateOverride&&typeof input.stateOverride==='object'?Promise.resolve(input.stateOverride):getState({parallel:true});
 try{return await runZuzuV6Agent({userPrompt,statePromise,selectedEventId:input?.selectedEventId,flowTrace,conversationHistory:arr(input?.conversationHistory).slice(-20),voiceConversation:input?.voiceConversation===true,usuarioLogado:input?.usuarioLogado,user:input?.user,authUser:input?.authUser,ce_acceso:input?.ce_acceso,clientNowIso:input?.clientNowIso,clientLocalDateTime:input?.clientLocalDateTime,clientTimeZone:input?.clientTimeZone,conversationLanguage:trim(input?.conversationLanguage)||'es-ES',externalSignal:input?.externalSignal,conversationId:input?.conversationId});}
 catch(error){const usage=summarizeGeminiUsageFromTrace(flowTrace);zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'V6 · FALLO SIN FALLBACK','KO',cleanGeminiError(error));return{ok:true,rejected:false,title:'Zuzu v6',answer:'La ruta v6 no ha podido cerrar este turno con un contrato o una ejecución válida. No he recurrido a ningún motor anterior.',spokenAnswer:'La ruta v6 no ha podido cerrar este turno.',warnings:['v6_execution_failure',cleanGeminiError(error)],charts:[],tables:[],files:[],provider:'zuzu-v6_1-exp-structured-output-failure',model:'',interactionId:'',conversationId:trim(input?.conversationId),meta:{version:'v6_1-exp',architecture:'v6_1-exp · CLEAN FAIL · GEMINI STRUCTURED OUTPUT CONTRACT · NO LEGACY FALLBACK',resetInteractionId:true,performance:{interactionCalls:num(usage?.calls)},geminiUsageEstimate:usage,debugTrace:flowTrace},debugTrace:flowTrace,showDebugTrace:true};}
}


function vnextP125ExtractDialogueJson(raw=''){const text0=trim(raw).replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i,'').replace(/\s*```$/,'').trim();let parsed={};try{parsed=parsePlanJsonLenientHf37(text0).parsed||{};}catch(_){}if(trim(parsed?.utterance))return parsed;try{const j=JSON.parse(text0);if(j&&typeof j==='object')return j;}catch(_){}const m=text0.match(/"utterance"\s*:\s*"((?:\\.|[^"\\])*)"/s);if(m){try{return{utterance:JSON.parse(`"${m[1]}"`)}}catch(_){}}return{};}
export async function generateZuzuItvDialogueUserTurn({mission='',conversationHistory=[],turnNumber=1,seed='',externalSignal=null}={}){
  const flowTrace=[],model=(configuredGeminiModelsForTask('zuzu-structured')[0]||'gemini-2.5-flash-lite'),hist=arr(conversationHistory).slice(-18),last=hist[hist.length-1]||{},state=vnextP123DialogueState(hist);
  const presentLine=h=>{const p=h?.presentationEvidence||{},tabs=arr(p?.tables),sort=arr(p?.viewSort).map(x=>`${trim(x?.field)} ${trim(x?.direction)==='desc'?'desc':'asc'}`).filter(Boolean);if(!num(p?.tableCount)&&!num(p?.chartCount))return'Presentación materializada: ninguna.';const tt=tabs.slice(0,3).map(t=>`${trim(t?.title||t?.key)||'tabla'} [${num(t?.rowCount)} filas; ${arr(t?.columns).slice(0,8).map(trim).filter(Boolean).join(', ')||'columnas no detalladas'}]`).join(' | ');return`Presentación materializada: ${num(p?.tableCount)} tabla(s)${tt?` → ${tt}`:''}${sort.length?` · orden=${sort.join(', ')}`:''}${num(p?.chartCount)?` · ${num(p.chartCount)} gráfica(s)`:''}.`;};
  const recent=hist.slice(-8),transcript=recent.map((h,i)=>`T${Math.max(1,turnNumber-recent.length+i)} Usuario: ${trim(h?.user)}\nZuzu: ${trim(h?.assistant)}\n${presentLine(h)}\nTools ejecutadas: ${arr(h?.tools).map(trim).filter(Boolean).join(', ')||'ninguna'}.`).join('\n\n');
  const input=`Eres el USUARIO SINTÉTICO de una ITV conversacional de Zuzu. No eres Zuzu. Continúa una conversación real e imprevisible siguiendo lo que acaba de ocurrir, no un guion. Puedes seguir, corregir, aclarar, bromear, manipular el objeto activo, volver atrás o cambiar de foco cuando sea natural.

MISIÓN GENERAL:
${trim(mission).slice(0,1800)}

ESTADO ESTRUCTURADO ACTUAL:
${JSON.stringify(state)}

TRANSCRIPT RECIENTE (incluye la PRESENTACIÓN REAL que el usuario habría visto):
${transcript||'(inicio)'}

ÚLTIMA RESPUESTA:
${trim(last?.assistant)||'(ninguna)'}
${presentLine(last)}

REGLAS DE EVALUACIÓN:
- Juzga lo que el usuario REAL habría visto: texto + tablas/gráficas materializadas + tools, no solo la frase de Zuzu.
- Si hay una tabla materializada, NO marques empty_promise solo porque el texto diga «te dejo la tabla» sin recitar sus filas. Si la tabla es incorrecta, marca previous_coherent=false y explica el contenido que falla.
- empty_promise=true solo cuando Zuzu anuncia una acción y no existe ejecución ni artefacto que la materialice.
- Cuando manipules una tabla concreta, pide columnas que aparezcan en la PRESENTACIÓN REAL de esa tabla. Solo prueba deliberadamente una columna inexistente de forma excepcional y acepta una aclaración correcta.
- No afirmes que una tabla sigue sin ordenar si la evidencia estructurada indica el orden solicitado; si la evidencia no permite comprobarlo, no inventes el fallo.
- table_action y memory_action implican requires_tool=true. Si pides buscar, ordenar, filtrar, cambiar columnas o consultar datos factuales, marca requires_tool=true.
- No reinicies la conversación para facilitar la prueba; sigue el foco actual o corrígelo como una persona.

Devuelve SOLO JSON:
{"utterance":"siguiente mensaje","move":"follow_current|clarify|correct|change_focus|casual|return_previous|table_action|memory_action","requires_tool":true|false,"change_focus":true|false,"target":"objeto/foco breve","assessment":{"previous_coherent":true|false,"focus_preserved":true|false,"empty_promise":true|false,"note":"máximo 180 caracteres"}}
Semilla: ${trim(seed)||'—'}.`;
  const started=Date.now(),apiKey=geminiKey();if(!apiKey)throw Object.assign(new Error('Falta GEMINI_API_KEY para el usuario sintético ITV.'),{status:503});
  // V3.15 TARGET25 · el simulador libre usa generateContent v1beta. Interactions v1 rechaza
  // developer/system instruction en este uso y convertía los 10 turnos libres en KO técnicos.
  const simUrl=`https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(model)}:generateContent`;
  const simBody={systemInstruction:{parts:[{text:'Simula un usuario humano para una prueba de continuidad. Evalúa también los artefactos estructurados materializados. Devuelve solo el JSON solicitado.'}]},contents:[{role:'user',parts:[{text:input}]}],generationConfig:{responseMimeType:'application/json',temperature:.35,maxOutputTokens:260,thinkingConfig:{thinkingBudget:0}}};
  let simRes,simPayload;try{({res:simRes,payload:simPayload}=await geminiFetchJsonWithTimeout(simUrl,simBody,apiKey,Number(process.env.CONTROLEVENT_ZUZU_ITV_SIMULATOR_TIMEOUT_MS||22000),externalSignal));}
  catch(error){zuzuTracePush(flowTrace,'ITV P1.26 · usuario sintético adaptativo · v1beta','KO',cleanGeminiError(error),{model});throw error;}
